### [1] [VFold: Symmetry-Aware Cross-Layer Value Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12338)
*Neha Verma,Sungwon Kim,Kenton Murray,Kevin Duh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VFold 提出对称性感知的跨层 value cache 合并策略，在不改动 LLM 架构、不显著增加解码开销的前提下压缩 KV cache 显存，并可与量化或 key cache 剪枝叠加获得更高压缩比，面向长上下文内存受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码时 KV cache 内存占用过大，而已有跨层缓存压缩方法往往需要修改模型架构并带来显著额外开销。

Method: 论文针对自回归 LLM 解码过程中的 KV cache：每层为每个已生成 token 保存 key 与 value 状态，长上下文下该缓存主导显存占用。方法基于跨层 value 状态之间的相似性（对称性）进行合并：在解码时识别并合并在层间冗余的 value 向量，从而在不改变 LLM 架构、不增加显著解码开销的情况下缩减 value cache 内存。该方法被设计为与既有缓存压缩手段正交：可与高比率量化或 key cache 剪枝叠加使用，使组合后的压缩比超过任一单一方法，且合并带来的额外计算/存储成本很小。摘要未披露具体的相似性度量、合并粒度（层对/层组/通道）、选择准则、是否微调、训练目标或推理吞吐/延迟数据，因此端到端模块顺序、数据流与优化目标的细节不可得。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出对称性感知（symmetry-aware）的 value cache 合并策略，在不改动架构、不引入显著解码开销的前提下降低缓存内存；证明该方法可与现有压缩技术（高比率量化、key cache 剪枝）正交组合，达到单一方法无法企及的压缩比，且额外成本极小；揭示 value cache 中存在大量未被利用的跨层冗余容量

Abstract: While caching key-value (KV) states accelerates Large Language Model (LLM) decoding, this cache can dominate memory usage at long context lengths. One solution is to compress this memory by exploiting inter-layer cache similarities. However, most existing techniques necessitate architectural changes to LLMs and incur substantial overhead. In this work, we propose a symmetry-aware value cache merging strategy that reduces cache memory while avoiding both harmful performance degradation and architectural overhead during decoding. Furthermore, we show that this approach can be exploited alongside existing cache compression techniques, composing with high-ratio quantization or key cache pruning to reach compression ratios that neither method reaches alone, with minimal additional cost. Ultimately, our findings reveal a major source of underutilized capacity in the value cache, offering a simple yet highly effective direction for scaling context windows under memory constraints.

</details>


### [2] [TokenRouter: Efficient Serving System for Token-Level LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12242)
*Tianyu Fu,Tengxuan Liu,Ruoxi Wang,Yixin Dong,Yi Ge,Yichen You,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenRouter 是一个面向 token 级 LLM 路由的高效服务系统，通过请求中心编程与模型中心执行、为每个 LLM 启动异步子服务器及延迟批处理调度，显著提升解码吞吐量（2.01-64.15x），为多模型路由推理提供实用系统方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 服务系统基于单一模型假设，在 token 级路由场景下会导致严重的步调不同步和频繁的批处理准入延迟，且实现复杂度高，难以高效服务细粒度路由推理。

Method: TokenRouter 遵循“请求中心编程，模型中心执行”的设计原则。开发者以单个请求的视角描述路由逻辑；运行时为每个 LLM 启动一个子服务器，并异步将请求分发到对应子服务器。每个子服务器采用延迟批处理调度器，其最优超参数从系统吞吐量的数学模型中推导得到。这种设计将编程模型与执行模型解耦，使得 token 级路由可以在不引入严重步调不同步与批处理准入延迟的情况下高效执行，同时降低开发者的实现复杂度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解码吞吐量", "value": "2.01-64.15x higher decoding throughput", "setting": "多种路由算法、工作负载和模型对下与现有系统对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 请求中心编程、模型中心执行原则：开发者从单个请求角度描述路由逻辑，运行时为每个 LLM 启动子服务器并异步分发请求。；延迟批处理调度器（delayed-batching scheduler），其超参数通过系统吞吐量数学模型推导得到最优值。；面向 token 级路由的专用服务架构，解决了步调不同步和批处理准入延迟问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing distributes inference work across different models, advancing the cost-quality Pareto frontier of LLM serving. While coarse-grained routing at the session or query level has been widely adopted in production systems, recent algorithmic work shows that fine-grained token-level routing can yield substantial efficiency and quality gains. However, efficiently serving token-level routed inference poses significant challenges to existing systems. Built on single-LLM assumptions, current systems suffer from severe step desynchronization and frequent batch admission delays under token-level routing, and they also impose high implementation complexity on developers. To address these challenges, we design TokenRouter, an efficient and developer-friendly serving system for token-level routed LLM inference. TokenRouter follows the principle of request-centric programming, model-centric execution: developers describe routing logic from the perspective of a single request, while the runtime launches a subserver for each LLM and dispatches requests asynchronously. Each subserver employs a delayed-batching scheduler, whose optimal hyperparameters are derived from a mathematical throughput model of the system. Across diverse routing algorithms, workloads, and model pairs, TokenRouter achieves 2.01-64.15x higher decoding throughput than existing systems, substantially advancing the serving efficiency of token-level LLM routing. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [3] [Specialized Decision Models vs. General-Purpose LLMs: Benchmarking Jev Across Knowledge, Reasoning, and Multilingual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11978)
*Xing Li,Qingcheng Chang,Jinzhong Ning,Changfeng Xu,Shenlong Zhang,Yijia Zhang,Ling Luo,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文评测专门化决策模型Jev与19个通用LLM在13个多选基准上的表现，发现其在知识/常识任务上可媲美前沿LLM并在MMLU-Redux、ARC-Challenge上最优，但在数学文字题上显著落后，说明专门决策模型适合知识型决策而非多步计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专门化的决策模型（只返回选项而非生成文本）能否在知识、推理和多语言任务上匹配通用大语言模型。

Method: Jev被描述为一种“系统1”决策模型，其输出形式为在给定选项中返回一个选择，而不是生成自由文本。论文未披露该模型的内部架构、参数规模、训练数据或推理硬件细节。评测流程为：在13个多选基准（覆盖知识、推理、多语言理解）上运行Jev，并与19个通用大语言模型进行对比，这些LLM分为前沿、代表和小型三个层级。评测指标为各基准上的准确率或得分，论文报告了Jev在知识/常识类基准上可媲美前沿LLM，在MMLU-Redux和ARC-Challenge上取得最佳分数，但在数学文字题（如MathQA）上表现较差。由于摘要未提供架构与训练细节，无法进一步描述其模块、数据流或优化目标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MMLU-Redux", "value": "最佳分数", "setting": "与19个LLM（前沿/代表/小型三层级）在13个多选基准对比中取得最高分"}, {"metric": "ARC-Challenge", "value": "最佳分数", "setting": "同上对比设置中取得最高分"}, {"metric": "MathQA", "value": "低于前沿中位数17.7分", "setting": "数学文字题基准，且得分低于所有19个对比LLM"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并系统评测Jev这一“系统1”式决策模型，输出为给定选项中的选择而非自由文本生成；在13个多选基准上与19个三个层级（前沿、代表、小型）的LLM进行对比；揭示专门决策模型在知识依赖型任务上可媲美前沿LLM，但在多步数学计算任务上显著落后

Abstract: Jev is a "System One" model that returns a choice among given options instead of generating text. We study how such a specialized decision model compares with general-purpose large language models (LLMs). We evaluate Jev on 13 multiple-choice benchmarks covering knowledge, reasoning, and multilingual understanding, and compare it with 19 LLMs in three tiers: frontier, representative, and small. Jev is competitive with frontier LLMs on knowledge and commonsense benchmarks and obtains the best score on MMLU-Redux and ARC-Challenge. Outside mathematics, it also outperforms most representative LLMs and all small LLMs. However, it falls behind on mathematical word problems: on MathQA, it is 17.7 points below the frontier median and scores lower than all 19 LLMs. These results indicate that a specialized decision model can match general-purpose LLMs on decisions that rely mainly on knowledge, but not on decisions that require multi-step calculation.

</details>


### [4] [Event-Centric Memory with Query-Aware Graph Augmentation for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11920)
*Yichen Liu,Chunfeng Yuan,Haowei Liu,Wenjuan Li,Zefeng Lin,Bing Li,Xu Chen,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QGMem 提出事件中心记忆框架：把长对话历史转为原子记忆单元并整合为动态记忆痕迹，查询时用混合检索与查询感知重排激活紧凑工作记忆，并将工作记忆建为局部图、编码成 graph token 注入 LLM 以支持关系推理与冲突消解；在六个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期对话记忆系统存在两难：扁平化记忆轻量但事件关系与状态更新隐式，图记忆显式建模结构但构造成本高且长历史中引入无关关系；需要兼顾结构化记忆能力与检索/推理效率。

Method: QGMem 是一个受人类记忆启发的记忆构建与激活框架。输入为长期对话历史与查询。构建阶段：先将长对话历史转换为事件索引的原子记忆单元，每个单元保留单个经验片段；再将相关原子单元整合为动态记忆痕迹，记忆痕迹保留状态轨迹与当前状态。检索阶段：当查询到来时，先进行混合记忆检索，从不同记忆表示中收集互补候选记忆；再对候选进行查询感知重排，激活最相关单元，形成紧凑的工作记忆。推理阶段：将工作记忆组织为局部图，以显式暴露其中的关系依赖，然后将该局部图编码为 graph token，与文本形式的工作记忆一起输入 LLM，帮助模型在答案生成时更好地利用证据并支持冲突感知推理。训练目标与优化细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索、多跳证据组合、冲突解决、超长对话推理性能", "value": "在六个基准上取得一致提升，并保持紧凑上下文与适中推理成本", "setting": "六个基准上的实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将长对话历史转换为事件索引的原子记忆单元，保留单个经验片段；将相关原子单元整合为动态记忆痕迹，保留状态轨迹与当前状态；查询到来时采用混合记忆检索获取互补候选记忆；通过查询感知重排激活最相关单元，形成紧凑的工作记忆；将工作记忆组织为局部图，编码为 graph token 与文本工作记忆共同提供给 LLM，支持关系依赖与冲突感知推理

Abstract: For persistent and personalized conversational agents, memory systems can enable them to remember, update, and reason over long histories by storing past interactions and retrieving relevant information. Existing memory systems typically follow two paradigms: flat-structured memory and graph-based memory. The former is lightweight but leaves event relations and state updates implicit, while the latter explicitly models memory structure but incurs additional construction cost and introduces irrelevant relations over long histories. To address these limitations, we propose QGMem, a novel memory construction and activation framework motivated by human memory, in which experience is organized into events and query-relevant events are modeled by graph as working memory. QGMem converts long dialogue histories into event-indexed atomic memory units that preserve individual experiences and consolidates related units into dynamic memory traces that retain state trajectories and current states. When a query arrives, hybrid memory retrieval gathers complementary candidate memories, and query-aware reranking activates the most relevant units as a compact working memory. To expose relational dependencies in the working memory and support conflict-aware reasoning, QGMem organizes the working memory as a local graph, which is then encoded as a graph token and provided to the LLM together with the textual working memory to improve evidence utilization during answer generation. Experiments across six benchmarks validate the framework and show consistent gains in retrieval, multi-hop evidence composition, conflict resolution, and ultra-long dialogue reasoning with compact contexts and moderate inference cost.

</details>


### [5] [DPPM: Dual-Path Parametric Memory for Personalized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11776)
*Yuhao Chen,Shuochen Liu,Jiayao Shi,Jian Hong,Chen Cheng,Xinyun Ding,Tao Wang,Ya Li,Quan Liu,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DPPM提出双路径参数化记忆，通过证据路径和增量路径融合生成历史条件LoRA适配器，提升跨会话个性化语言模型性能，在PersonaMem-v2和PrefEval上取得优于基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨会话个性化中，参数化记忆难以集成跨会话证据且循环更新会衰减早期信息。

Method: DPPM由两条路径构成：证据路径直接对交互历史的表示进行池化以保留早期证据；增量路径顺序更新关联状态以捕获变化。两条路径的输出融合后生成历史条件LoRA适配器，该适配器结合了证据累积与有序修订，用于条件化语言模型的生成。未见更具体的模块、数据流或训练目标细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "PersonaMem-v2", "value": "54.22%", "setting": "与基线对比，多骨干网络"}, {"metric": "PrefEval", "value": "86.79%", "setting": "与基线对比，多骨干网络"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出双路径参数化记忆（DPPM），包含证据路径（直接池化交互历史表示）与增量路径（关联状态顺序更新）；通过融合两条路径生成历史条件LoRA适配器，实现证据累积与有序修订的结合

Abstract: Long-term personalization requires language models to use interaction history to track users' preferences across sessions. Parametric memory encodes this interaction history into model parameters or adapters, reducing the need to include it in the inference context. However, independent context compilation leaves cross-session integration unspecified, while recurrent updates can attenuate earlier evidence. To address these challenges, we propose Dual-Path Parametric Memory (DPPM). Its Evidence path directly pools representations of the interaction history to preserve earlier evidence, while its Delta path sequentially updates an associative state to capture changes. Fusing both outputs produces history-conditioned LoRA adapters that combine evidence accumulation with ordered revision. Across multiple backbones, DPPM outperforms the evaluated baselines, achieving 54.22% on PersonaMem-v2 and 86.79% on PrefEval. These results suggest that DPPM provides a simple and effective design choice for cross-session personalized parametric memory.

</details>


### [6] [Thinking Inertia: LLMs Keep Thinking When Told Not To](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11765)
*Dianqiao Lei,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Pan Lu,Philip Torr,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM“不思考”行为，提出三层评估指标，发现显式无思考控制无法可靠消除可见推理，存在“Thinking Inertia”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何可靠地测量和实现LLM的“不思考”行为，以及显式控制能否消除可见推理。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是围绕现有LLM的响应评估与提示干预展开。输入为问题与提示干预，模型输出被归一化为预答案轨迹（pre-answer trace）和最终答案。评估流程包括三个层次：Empty-Thinking Rate衡量严格仅答案输出；Question-Pre-answer Relevance衡量问题与预答案轨迹的相似度；LLM-as-judge Explicit Inference Rate衡量可见显式推理。实验覆盖六个LLM与六种提示干预，在Boolean、多选、开放问答任务上进行，并比较不同答案空间下仅答案合规性与任务准确率的权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将响应归一化为预答案轨迹和最终答案，并从三个层面评估不思考：Empty-Thinking Rate、指令感知的Question-Pre-answer Relevance、LLM-as-judge Explicit Inference Rate；提出并验证“Thinking Inertia”现象：显式无思考控制无法可靠消除可见推理，且随答案空间开放而更普遍；跨6个LLM、6种提示干预、Boolean/多选/开放问答的系统性实证研究

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly ship with explicit "thinking modes", yet their counterpart, "no-thinking", has received far less attention. We study LLMs' no-thinking behavior along two axes. a. How to measure no-thinking? Prior work typically defines no-thinking through proxies such as a disabled thinking mode or the absence of long traces. These proxies are unreliable: disabled thinking modes may still emit reasoning, while long traces may contain filler rather than genuine inference. We instead normalize each response into a pre-answer trace and final answer, and evaluate it at three levels: (i) Empty-Thinking Rate for strict answer-only compliance; (ii) instruction-aware Question-Pre-answer Relevance for similarity between the question and pre-answer trace; and (iii) LLM-as-judge Explicit Inference Rate for visible explicit inference. Together, these metrics distinguish answer-only output, relevant but non-inferential text, and explicit inference. b. How does no-thinking vary across tasks and models? We evaluate six prompting interventions on six LLMs across Boolean, multiple-choice, and open-ended questions. We find that explicit no-think controls cannot reliably eliminate visible inference. Models instead exhibit "Thinking Inertia": explicit inference persists even under strict controls and becomes more prevalent as the answer space opens. Accuracy remains stable on Boolean and multiple-choice tasks, whereas open-ended tasks reveal a trade-off between answer-only compliance and task accuracy. Rewriting the same questions across answer spaces shows that supplying candidate answers makes answer-only responses easier to produce. These findings establish no-thinking as a non-trivial capability: stopping explicit reasoning cannot be assumed from model settings or instructions alone and deserves systematic evaluation alongside reasoning ability.

</details>


### [7] [Smoothing the Top-k Exposure Boundary for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11575)
*Yunkai Chai,Tong Zhu,Xiaoye Qu,Xuyang Hu,Guanjie Chen,Qipeng Guo,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Elastic Expert Routing，通过在训练时以k为中心随机采样激活专家预算，把稀疏MoE的top-k硬边界软化为概率分布，保持期望计算成本不变，在SFT和从头预训练上均取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏MoE训练强制静态top-k专家选择，将连续路由分布转化为刚性阶跃函数，在竞争激烈的专家间形成脆弱边界，导致轻微分数波动就决定专家进入全监督或零反馈区域。

Method: 论文针对稀疏MoE模型的路由机制提出Elastic Expert Routing。输入为token表示，路由器输出各专家的连续得分分布。传统方法静态选择top-k专家，形成硬边界。作者改为从以k为中心的局部离散分布中随机采样当前迭代的激活专家预算，从而在多个训练迭代上把硬阈值平滑为渐进概率分布。由于采样邻域关于k对称，期望激活专家数与固定top-k相同，因此训练与推理计算预算不变。训练目标仍为原有任务损失（SFT或预训练语言建模），路由采样仅改变每步激活的专家数量。推理时保持既定预算。论文在OLMoE-1B-7B和Qwen3-30B-A3B上做SFT，并在从头预训练设置下与静态top-k对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "下游宏平均", "value": "+0.84", "setting": "OLMoE-1B-7B 监督微调"}, {"metric": "下游宏平均", "value": "+2.02", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B 监督微调"}, {"metric": "下游任务平均", "value": "+1.6", "setting": "从头预训练，对比静态top-k基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Elastic Expert Routing：从以k为中心的局部离散分布中随机采样激活专家预算，随训练迭代将尖锐阈值软化为渐变概率分布；采样邻域对称，使期望计算成本与确定性top-k训练匹配，同时保持推理预算不变；在监督微调和从头预训练两种设置下验证该路由机制的有效性

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts models scale parameter capacity efficiently while maintaining a fixed compute budget per token. However, traditional training paradigms enforce a static choice of top-$k$ experts, which converts a continuous routing distribution into a rigid step function. This constraint introduces a brittle boundary where highly competitive experts are arbitrarily separated into full-supervision and zero-feedback zones based on minor score fluctuations. To address this issue, we propose Elastic Expert Routing, which stochastically samples the active expert budget from a localized discrete distribution centered at $k$. Over multiple training iterations, this mechanism softens the sharp threshold into a gradual probability distribution. Because the sampling neighborhood remains symmetric, this approach matches the expected computational cost of deterministic training, while preserving the inference budget. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of our method on both supervised fine-tuning and from-scratch pretraining settings. During supervised fine-tuning, elastic routing improves downstream macro-averages on OLMoE-1B-7B and Qwen3-30B-A3B by $+0.84$ and $+2.02$ points, respectively. In addition, in from-scratch pretraining, it outperforms the static top-$k$ baseline by $1.6$ points on average across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [8] [Incremental Open-Ended Deep Research with Structured Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11566)
*Meilin Chen,Hongyuan Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Incremental-OEDR 任务与 Structured Harness 系统，用结构化 outline/section/evidence 表示与持久证据池实现深度研究报告的增量更新与证据复用，并给出十年跨度的单步与长链评估框架；在 DeepResearch Bench 上 ROUGE-L F1 最高提升 0.51、outline-level EM F1 提升 0.63，同时 token 降低 33%、搜索调用减少 61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Open-Ended Deep Research 系统从零生成报告，在需要随新信息持续维护报告的场景下效率低、连续性差、研究成本高。

Method: Structured Harness 将研究报告表示为由 outline、section 与 supporting evidence 组成的结构化集合。系统提供三个核心组件：结构化检索（针对报告结构进行检索而非无结构文本检索）、持久化结构化证据池（跨更新轮次保存并复用已获得的证据）、结构化生成（在结构约束下对报告进行选择性更新而非整体重写）。输入为已有报告状态与新增可用信息，数据流为：新信息进入→结构化检索获取补充证据→写入持久证据池→按 outline/section 粒度判断哪些部分需修订、哪些有效知识保留→结构化生成产出更新后的报告状态。评估侧构建覆盖十年的时间评估框架，包含评估单次状态转移的 Single-Step Task 与评估长期更新链的 Long-Chain Task，并在 DeepResearch Bench 与 DeepConsult 上以开源配置（OC）和专有配置（PC）两种设置进行实验。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "content-level ROUGE-L F1", "value": "up to +0.51", "setting": "DeepResearch Bench，对比 OEDR"}, {"metric": "outline-level EM F1", "value": "up to +0.63", "setting": "DeepResearch Bench，对比 OEDR"}, {"metric": "token consumption", "value": "降低 33%", "setting": "DeepResearch Bench，对比 OEDR"}, {"metric": "search calls", "value": "减少 61%", "setting": "DeepResearch Bench，对比 OEDR"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Incremental-OEDR 任务设置：把报告视为演进的研究状态，保留有效知识、修订过时/不完整内容、纳入新信息；提出 Structured Harness：将报告表示为 outline、section、supporting evidence 的结构化集合；结构化检索与持久化结构化证据池，支持证据复用与选择性报告更新；建立跨越十年的时间评估框架，含 Single-Step Task 与 Long-Chain Task

Abstract: Existing Open-Ended Deep Research (OEDR) systems primarily generate reports from scratch, making them inefficient for scenarios where research reports need to be continuously maintained as new information emerges. We introduce \textbf{Incremental Open-Ended Deep Research (Incremental-OEDR)}, a research setting that treats a report as an evolving research state and incrementally updates it by preserving valid knowledge, revising outdated or incomplete content, and incorporating newly available information. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{Structured Harness}, which represents reports as structured collections of outlines, sections, and supporting evidence, and provides structured retrieval, a persistent structured evidence pool, and structured generation for selective report updating and evidence reuse. We further establish a temporal evaluation framework spanning ten years, with \emph{Single-Step Task} and \emph{Long-Chain Task} to evaluate incremental updates over both individual transitions and long-term update chains. Extensive Experiments on DeepResearch Bench and DeepConsult under both the Open-source Configuration (OC) and Proprietary Configuration (PC) show that Incremental-OEDR maintains competitive report quality while substantially improving report continuity and reducing research costs. As shown in Figure~\ref{fig:profile}, it achieves up to 0.51 higher content-level ROUGE-L F1, 0.63 higher outline-level EM F1, 33\% lower token consumption, and 61\% fewer search calls than OEDR on DeepResearch Bench. For more details, please refer to our project page: this https URL .

</details>

*Vinko Sabolčec,Bettina Messmer,Yassine Turki,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将英语质量分类器扩展至超 100 种语言的多语言适配方法：在 Transformer 编码器嵌入上训练 MLP，以机器翻译文本的英语分类器得分为标签。1B/3B/8B 规模实验表明，方法保持下游 LLM 基准性能且不损害区域文化知识，分类器还能泛化到未见语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将已有的英语质量分类器适配到百余种语言，以在低资源语言预训练数据筛选中保持下游 LLM 性能，同时避免损害区域性文化知识。

Method: 输入为多语言文本，经仅编码器 Transformer 编码得到嵌入表示；在嵌入之上训练一个小型多层感知机（MLP）进行质量打分。训练标签由英语质量分类器对机器翻译后的文本打分提供，从而将英语分类器的评分标准迁移到多语言场景。推理时，多语言文本经编码器和 MLP 直接输出质量分数，用于筛选预训练数据。实验在 1B、3B、8B 参数规模的下游 LLM 上评估，并比对分类器对合成数据和网络样本的评分及与 LLM 评分的相关性。

Conclusion: 提出在仅编码器 Transformer 嵌入之上训练小型 MLP 的多语言适配方法；使用英语质量分类器对机器翻译文本打分为标签，实现跨语言监督信号迁移；在 1B、3B、8B 规模上验证方法保持下游 LLM 基准性能，不损害区域文化知识；通过高质量合成数据与网络样本的分数对比及与 LLM 评分的相关性分析，揭示跨语言泛化能力

Abstract: Recent advances in large language model (LLM) pretraining highlight the role of high-quality training data in improving performance. While model-based filtering has proven effective in selecting high-quality subsets from web-scale corpora, especially for high-resource languages, low-resource languages face challenges due to limited availability of annotated data. This work explores extending quality filtering to over 100 languages by proposing a multilingual adaptation approach that converts an existing English quality classifier into a multilingual variant. Our approach proposes training a small multi-layer perceptron on top of Transformer encoder-only model embeddings, using multilingual text as input and scores obtained from English classifiers applied to machine-translated text as labels. Our 1B, 3B and 8B scale experiments show that our approach maintains the downstream LLM benchmark performance of existing multilingual model-based filtering baselines, without harming regional and cultural knowledge benchmarks. To further evaluate cross-lingual generalization, we compare classifier scores of high-quality synthetic data and web samples, and the correlation of classifier scores with LLM-based ones, revealing that the classifier can learn the scoring criteria of its original English variant, even for languages not included in its training data.

</details>


### [10] [SWE-Journey: Towards More Realistic Evaluation of Coding Assistants through Long-Horizon, Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11559)
*Hexuan Deng,Yue Wang,Wenyu Jiang,Cheng Yang,Haolin Yang,Zhaohua Zhang,Chenchen Zhao,Beiduo Chen,Muxi Chen,Sa Zhu,Geyuan Zhu,Jianhuan Zhuo,Qiuyong Xiao,Tianwen Jiang,Jihong Zhang,Xuebo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SWE-Journey 是一个更贴近真实的编码助手评测基准，通过 weak-to-strong 合成流水线构造长时程任务，并用从真实数据挖掘的四类用户画像驱动的用户模拟智能体实现多轮交互评测。结果显示模型在 software architects 画像下测试通过率超过 75%，而在 non-coders 画像下不足 25%，并指出 asking right、finding right、fixing right 是交互中的关键能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码助手基准在任务时程和交互长度上严重偏离真实使用场景，无法衡量编码助手在长链条开发任务中、面对不同用户画像的多轮澄清与迭代下的真实表现。

Method: 论文提出 SWE-Journey 评测框架，包含两个主要构造模块。第一，针对任务时程差距，设计 weak-to-strong 合成流水线，自动从较弱信号出发构造长时程编码任务，使任务需要跨越较长的连续开发工作链。第二，针对交互差距，从真实交互数据中挖掘四类代表性用户画像，并据此构建用户模拟智能体，在与被测编码助手的多轮对话中扮演不同背景用户，复现需求澄清与实现调整的真实交互过程。评测时，被测编码助手在与用户模拟智能体的多轮交互中，于持续演进的代码库上完成被请求的功能开发；随后通过测试套件衡量是否通过被请求功能的测试。论文统计不同用户画像下的测试通过率，并对失败原因进行分析，归纳出 asking right（正确提问澄清需求）、finding right（定位正确代码与信息）、fixing right（正确修复实现）三类关键交互能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "测试通过率", "value": "超过 75%（software architects 用户画像）", "setting": "SWE-Journey 基准，用户模拟智能体为 software architects 画像的多轮交互评测"}, {"metric": "测试通过率", "value": "不足 25%（non-coders 用户画像）", "setting": "SWE-Journey 基准，用户模拟智能体为 non-coders 画像的多轮交互评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 weak-to-strong 合成流水线，自动构造长时程（long-horizon）编码任务，解决任务时程差距；从真实交互数据中挖掘四类代表性用户画像，并构建用户模拟智能体以复现真实编码协助交互，解决交互长度差距；基于 SWE-Journey 的评测揭示模型能力对用户画像的强烈依赖：software architects 场景测试通过率超过 75%，而 non-coders 场景不足 25%，并归纳出 asking right、finding right、fixing right 三项关键交互能力

Abstract: Coding assistants such as Claude Code and Codex have become a major application of LLM agents, yet existing benchmarks remain far from real-world use, particularly in task horizon and interaction length. Code assistants require completing long chains of development work in continuously evolving repositories, while repeatedly clarifying requirements and adapting implementations through multi-turn interaction. To address these gaps, we introduce SWE-Journey, a benchmark for more realistic evaluation of coding assistants. To address the task-horizon gap, we propose a weak-to-strong synthesis pipeline that automatically constructs long-horizon coding tasks. To address the interaction gap, we mine four representative user personas from real interaction data and build a user-simulation agent to reproduce realistic code-assistance interactions. On average, models pass over 75% of tests for requested functionality with software architects, but fewer than 25% with non-coders. These results show that current coding assistants still fall short of enabling reliable coding for non-coders. We further analyze the reasons for this gap and identify asking right, finding right, and fixing right as key capabilities during interaction.

</details>


### [11] [Residual Advantage: Student-Relative Teacher Guidance for RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11519)
*Xiaobing Chen,Zhiqi Pang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Residual Advantage：把教师-学生的token级概率残差转成有界的一步优势，并在响应内中心化后与验证器优势相加，使教师只重分配步内信用而不改变回答级标签；CoRA 进一步用验证器优势迭代更新教师 LoRA。在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B 学生、Qwen3-8B 教师上，结合 GRPO/REINFORCE++ 在三个数学基准的 24 组对比中全部优于底层算法，macro Avg@8 提升 1.7–3.6 分、Pass@8 提升 3.9–6.3 分，并超过 teacher-only OPD，CoRA 再增 1.0–1.5 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR只给整条回答一个结果标签，无法为内部推理步骤分配信用；OPD虽提供token级指导，但其逐点log-ratio信号无界且不反映教师-学生在整个词表上的分歧模式，强解题教师未必是合适的引导者。

Method: 方法以标准策略梯度框架为基础。输入为提示与采样得到的响应序列，训练信号由两部分相加：(1) 验证器优势——由可验证奖励（如数学答案是否正确）给出的单条响应级结果标签，对应整条响应的优势；(2) 教师引导项——在策略采样到的前缀处，计算教师模型与当前学生模型对下一个token的概率残差（log-ratio形式），将其视为有界的一步奖励，再减去学生策略在该状态下的价值估计，得到标准的一步优势；随后在每条响应内部对该引导优势做中心化（减去响应内均值），使其均值为零。这一中心化保证引导项不会改变响应的总体标签均值，教师仅在各步骤之间重新分配信用。两部分相加后的总优势送入 GRPO 或 REINFORCE++ 等序列优势算法更新学生策略。CoRA 变体进一步在同一批已由验证器打分的 学生样本上，用验证器优势更新一个教师 LoRA，并在下一轮迭代中用更新后的教师计算残差引导，从而让引导信号适应学生当前的能力与解题路径。整个流程为迭代式在线采样-打分-更新循环，学生与教师（CoRA中的LoRA）交替更新。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro Avg@8", "value": "提升 1.7–3.6 分", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B-Base 与 Qwen3-4B-Base 作学生、Qwen3-8B 作教师，RA 结合 GRPO 或 REINFORCE++，在三个数学基准上全部 24 组对比均优于底层序列优势算法"}, {"metric": "Pass@8", "value": "提升 3.9–6.3 分", "setting": "同上设置"}, {"metric": "macro Avg@8", "value": "进一步提升 1.0–1.5 分", "setting": "CoRA 在 RA 基础上，教师 LoRA 随迭代更新"}, {"metric": "相对 teacher-only OPD 的表现", "value": "RA+GRPO 与 RA+REINFORCE++ 两种组合均超过 teacher-only OPD", "setting": "同上学生/教师配置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将教师-学生概率残差作为有界的一步奖励，减去学生策略下的状态价值形成标准优势；在每条回答内部做中心化，使引导项均值为零，保证验证器优势仍等于该回答的标签均值，教师只重新分配步内信用；CoRA：在同一批已打分的学生样本上用验证器优势更新教师LoRA，并在下一轮残差中使用更新后的教师，使引导随学生尝试自适应

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have become two main paradigms for post-training reasoning models. RLVR gives each response a single outcome label, leaving the steps inside it without separate credit. OPD provides token-level guidance at student-visited prefixes, but its pointwise signal does not directly reflect the pattern of teacher--student disagreement across the vocabulary. Dense, unbounded log-ratio supervision can amplify the teacher's influence, yet a strong solver is not necessarily a suitable guide when the student's solution paths depart from the teacher's. We propose Residual Advantage (\RA{}), which treats the teacher--student probability residual as a bounded one-step reward, subtracts the corresponding state value under the student policy to form a standard advantage, and centers the result within each response before adding it to the verifier advantage. The guidance term has zero mean within each response, so the verifier advantage remains the response's mean label and the teacher only redistributes credit among the steps within it. \CoRA{} further updates a teacher LoRA with verifier advantages on the same scored student batch and uses the updated teacher in the next iteration's residual, adapting guidance to the student's attempts. With Qwen3-1.7B-Base and Qwen3-4B-Base students and a Qwen3-8B teacher, \RA{} combined with GRPO or REINFORCE++ improves the underlying sequence-advantage algorithm in all 24 comparisons on three mathematical benchmarks, raising macro Avg@8 by 1.7--3.6 points and Pass@8 by 3.9--6.3 points. Both combinations surpass teacher-only OPD, and \CoRA{} adds a further 1.0--1.5 Avg@8 points.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Sequences: Distilling Structured Decision Memory for LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11501)
*Leikun Liang,Guoshuai Wang,Xingsheng He,Yushan Han,Yunyi Xuan,Xiaoxiao Xu,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MARI 用离线蒸馏的结构化决策记忆库替代扁平行为序列，通过检索相关 SDM 增强 LLM 推荐推理，在常规与困难选择预测上优于基线且延迟开销低，并提供可解释证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推荐方法将异质行为扁平化为同质 token 序列，忽略不同行为在决策中的语义层级与权衡关系，导致在高度相似物品的困难选择场景中性能下降。

Method: MARI 由离线记忆构建与在线检索增强推理两部分组成。离线阶段，以用户异质行为（如浏览、购买等）及用户生成内容为输入，通过 Post-Hoc Decision Distillation 生成结构化决策记忆（SDM），每条 SDM 是用户过去决策中目标、约束与权衡的简洁记录；这些 SDM 被归档到 Decision Memory Bank（DMB）。在线阶段，系统根据当前推荐上下文从 DMB 中检索相关的 SDM，将其作为显式决策证据注入 LLM 提示，增强 LLM 对候选物品的推理与排序；由于记忆构建与在线推理解耦，在线只需检索与轻量推理，避免了处理长原始行为序列的开销。训练目标为标准的下一物品预测，并在新提出的 Difficult Choice Prediction 任务上评估；论文未披露优化算法细节、模型参数规模、训练数据规模、硬件或延迟数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Decision Memory Bank (DMB)，存储用户过去推理依据形成的结构化决策记忆 (SDM)，包含目标、约束与权衡。；通过 Post-Hoc Decision Distillation 从异质行为与用户生成内容中离线生成 SDM，将长序列压缩为可检索的结构化证据。；在线推理时检索相关 SDM 增强 LLM 推理，实现可解释性与可扩展性，同时避免处理长原始序列的高成本。；引入 Difficult Choice Prediction 任务以评估密集权衡场景下的推荐能力。

Abstract: Despite the adoption of large language models (LLMs) in recommendation systems, prevailing approaches mostly model single-type behaviors (e.g., views or purchases). Even when incorporating multiple behaviors, existing methods flatten heterogeneous actions into homogeneous token sequences, ignoring their distinct decision-making roles. This flattening fails to capture semantic hierarchies and contextual nuances in complex decision-making, such as trade-offs between price and quality. Consequently, performance degrades in critical ``difficult-choice'' scenarios involving highly similar items. To bridge this gap, we propose MARI (Memory-Augmented Recommendation with Interpretability), which grounds predictions in explicit, structured decision evidence. MARI maintains a Decision Memory Bank (DMB) that archives users' past rationales as Structured Decision Memories (SDMs): concise records of goals, constraints, and trade-offs. These SDMs are generated offline via Post-Hoc Decision Distillation from heterogeneous behaviors and user-generated content. By retrieving relevant SDMs to augment LLM reasoning, MARI achieves interpretability and scalability without the prohibitive cost of processing long raw sequences. Extensive experiments show MARI significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard next-item prediction and a newly introduced Difficult Choice Prediction task, incurring low latency overhead by decoupling memory construction from online inference. Qualitative analyses reveal actionable, human-readable insights into user decision-making, marking a concrete step toward reasoning-aware recommendation systems.

</details>


### [13] [AdaptEvo: Adaptive Agent Learning with Evolving Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11354)
*Shijun Wan,Jiancong Xie,Hang Xu,Jin Duan,Qixiong Wang,Xi Xiang,Maofei Que,Yahui Liu,Zhongyu Wei,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaptEvo 面向规则约束的情境决策任务，提出 Confidence-Adaptive GRPO 按参考置信度平衡结果与过程奖励，并配合 Evolution 模块从反复失败中合成决策知识、精炼过程评分标准。基于工业多模态内容审核数据集的 In-Period 测试中，使用 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 取得 61.9% 精确标签准确率与 72.2% 二元决策准确率，分别超过 GRPO 7.5 和 3.7 个百分点；在规则变更后的 Out-of-Period 测试中，CA-GRPO 相对基座模型保持精确标签精度增益且优于固定奖励混合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规则文档存在指引空缺、参考标注与规则/证据支持程度不一致（不完美监督）的情况下，如何让模型稳定地应用规则并结合个案上下文与证据做出决策，且在规则变化后不出现性能退化。

Method: AdaptEvo 由三个部分协同构成。输入侧为规则文本、个案上下文与证据，以及参考判断；输出为精确标签与二元决策。Training 模块采用 Confidence-Adaptive GRPO（CA-GRPO）：以 GRPO 为基础，根据每条参考标注的置信度对结果奖励与过程奖励进行自适应加权，使高置信样本更多驱动结果对齐、低置信或过程存疑样本更多依赖过程评分，从而在不完美监督下稳定策略优化。Evolution 模块跨训练案例收集反复出现的失败模式，合成可复用的决策知识，并对过程评分标准（rubric）进行精炼，使评分器能够发现此前被忽视的过程性错误；精炼后的知识与评分标准回灌到训练奖励与后续迭代中，形成训练—演化闭环。评测侧构建工业多模态内容审核数据集，含训练集、In-Period 测试集与规则变更后采集的 Out-of-Period 测试集，用于检验规则漂移下的泛化。推理时策略模型直接依据规则与个案上下文输出判断，不依赖 Evolution 模块的在线介入，但其训练过程已内化演化得到的决策知识与评分标准。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "exact-label accuracy", "value": "61.9%（超过 GRPO 7.5 个百分点）", "setting": "Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，In-Period 测试集"}, {"metric": "binary decision accuracy", "value": "72.2%（超过 GRPO 3.7 个百分点）", "setting": "Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，In-Period 测试集"}, {"metric": "exact-label accuracy", "value": "在无注入决策知识的条件下，CA-GRPO 训练的策略在多个评测 checkpoint 上相对基座模型保持精确标签精度增益，而 GRPO 随训练继续下降", "setting": "Out-Period 测试集（规则变更后）"}, {"metric": "Out-of-Period 两项指标", "value": "CA-GRPO 优于所测试的固定奖励混合方案", "setting": "Out-of-Period 测试集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Confidence-Adaptive GRPO（CA-GRPO）：依据参考置信度在结果奖励与过程奖励之间进行自适应平衡的策略优化目标；Evolution 模块：从训练案例中反复出现的失败中合成可复用决策知识，并精炼过程评分标准以发现被忽视的错误；工业多模态内容审核数据集：包含训练集、In-Period 测试集与规则变更后采集的 Out-of-Period 测试集；在规则变更场景下证明显式决策知识注入的必要性：无知识注入时 CA-GRPO 仍保持精确标签精度增益，而 GRPO 随训练继续而下降

Abstract: Rule-governed contextual decision tasks require models to apply specified rules to case-specific context and evidence. Written rules can leave gaps in decision guidance and process evaluation, while reference judgments vary in their support from the rules and evidence. To address these challenges, we introduce AdaptEvo, a framework for learning under imperfect supervision that couples confidence-adaptive policy optimization with evolving decision knowledge and evaluation rubrics. Its Training module uses Confidence-Adaptive GRPO (CA-GRPO) to balance outcome and process rewards according to reference confidence. Its Evolution module synthesizes reusable decision knowledge from recurring failures across training cases and refines process rubrics to detect overlooked errors. To support empirical evaluation, we construct an industrial multimodal content moderation dataset comprising a training set and In-Period and Out-of-Period test sets, with the latter collected under changed rules. Using Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, AdaptEvo achieves 61.9% exact-label accuracy and 72.2% binary decision accuracy on In-Period, exceeding GRPO by 7.5 and 3.7 percentage points, respectively. On Out-of-Period, the policy trained with CA-GRPO retains exact-label accuracy gains over the base model across evaluated checkpoints without injected decision knowledge, while GRPO declines with continued training. CA-GRPO also outperforms the tested fixed reward mixtures on both Out-of-Period metrics.

</details>


### [14] [When Do We Need On-Policy Distillation? Distilling on Offline Student Rollouts Is Often Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11291)
*Siyan Zhao,Yonggan Fu,Jindong Jiang,Shih-Yang Liu,Song Bian,Byung-Kwan Lee,Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Wenliang Dai,Hanrong Ye,Aditya Grover,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统研究LLM蒸馏中on-policy采样是否总是更好，提出Semi-OPD方法，在17组模型对上14组超越OPD，最高+13.6%准确率与11.4倍训练加速，并给出基于教师-学生对齐的选择准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在教师-学生对之间，on-policy distillation是否总是优于使用初始学生离线rollout的蒸馏（Semi-OPD），以及何时应选择哪种方案。

Method: 论文聚焦于LLM后训练阶段的蒸馏算法，不涉及推理或生产系统架构。方法层面，Semi-OPD首先使用初始学生模型在数据集上生成离线rollout，然后在这些固定rollout上使用教师模型的logits进行蒸馏训练；而OPD则持续使用当前学生模型的on-policy rollout进行蒸馏。论文未披露模型具体架构、训练硬件或数据细节，仅通过输出token重叠率（overlap ratio）分析教师-学生对齐程度，并据此给出选择建议。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "在14/17组教师-学生对中Semi-OPD优于OPD，最高提升+13.6%", "setting": "17组教师-学生对，参数规模1.5B至235B"}, {"metric": "训练加速", "value": "最高11.4倍", "setting": "与OPD相比的训练速度提升"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Semi-OPD：在初始学生模型生成的离线rollout上蒸馏，而非在on-policy学生rollout上蒸馏；通过输出token重叠率量化教师-学生对齐程度，给出OPD与Semi-OPD的选择判据；揭示有效蒸馏需同时对教师和学生保持on-policy，并解释长上下文下学生rollout对教师逐渐off-policy的问题

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become increasingly popular for transferring teacher capabilities to student models. In this work, we ask a critical research question: Is on-policy sampling always beneficial for distilling arbitrary teacher-student pairs? We show that a simple alternative, Semi-OPD, which distills from offline rollouts generated by the initial student, can often outperform OPD in both accuracy and training efficiency. Across 17 teacher-student pairs ranging from 1.5B to 235B parameters, Semi-OPD outperforms OPD in 14 cases, with up to +13.6% accuracy and 11.4x training speedup. We further find that the choice between OPD and Semi-OPD depends on the alignment between the initial teacher and student, quantified by an output-token overlap ratio: OPD is beneficial only when the two are highly aligned with high overlap ratios. Our deeper investigation suggests that effective distillation requires on-policyness w.r.t. both the student and the teacher. For misaligned pairs, student rollouts can become increasingly off-policy w.r.t. the teacher as context length grows, weakening the distillation signal. In contrast, Semi-OPD is often more stable, as it distills on shorter contexts while covering full trajectories and exposing the student to more teacher-preferred tokens. Beyond proposing Semi-OPD as an efficient alternative, our work motivates the community to rethink when to use OPD and to study stronger OPD variants with meaningful teacher-student pairs.

</details>


### [15] [MetaEncoder: Exploring the Limit of Bi-Encoders for Multimodal System One Decision Making with Natural Language Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11316)
*Jianpeng Cheng,Guangyu Sun,Aashu Singh,Benyu Zhang,Haixing Dai,Hossein Mansour,Jiangfan Zhang,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Wei Sun,Xuanming Cui,Yanli Liu,Qi Guo,Max Xiangjun Fan,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MetaEncoder 将 30B 预训练解码器微调为自然语言接口下的决策编码器，用双向编码器与单向对比学习支持封闭集与百万级开放集的多模态决策与检索，在 11 个基准套件 190 个任务上评估，并与 SOTA 多模态编码器对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何用完全自然语言的 System One 接口，支持多模态辅助输入，并在从封闭小候选集到百万级开放候选集上高效完成请求-候选对齐与决策。

Method: MetaEncoder 以预训练 Muse-Glimmer 30B 解码器为骨干，通过微调将其转化为指令跟随的决策编码器。输入侧，用户请求与每个候选选项均以自然语言表达，并可附带图像/视频等多模态辅助信息；模型需要将这些异构输入编码为可用于对齐的表示。为同时支持小规模封闭集（<256）与百万级开放集，MetaEncoder 采用双向编码器结构：请求侧与候选侧分别编码，并通过单向对比学习进行训练，使请求表示与其正确候选表示在向量空间中接近。推理时，对封闭集可直接对少量候选打分；对开放集则利用双向编码器独立编码请求与候选，支持基于向量相似度的检索或匹配，避免对所有候选进行交叉编码。该设计旨在在保持多模态决策能力的同时，实现大规模候选空间下的可扩展检索与决策。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将预训练 Muse-Glimmer 30B 解码器微调为指令跟随的决策编码器（MetaEncoder），用于输出受约束决策而非自由文本生成。；采用双向编码器架构与单向对比学习（unidirectional contrastive learning）进行请求-候选对齐，以扩展到百万级开放候选集。；在统一自然语言界面下融合图像与视频等多模态辅助输入，覆盖多模态决策、理解（封闭集）与检索（开放集）。

Abstract: System One models output constrained decisions and probability distributions rather than free-form text generation. While prevailing paradigms rely on structured schema objects to encode state, intent, and candidate choices, we revisit a fully natural language-based System One interface. In this framework, both the user request and each candidate option are expressed in natural language, supported by multimodal (image and video) auxiliary inputs. We introduce MetaEncoder, which fine-tunes a pre-trained Muse-Glimmer 30B decoder into an instruction-following decision-making encoder. To scale effectively across both small closed-set (< 256) and massive open-set (millions) candidate spaces, MetaEncoder employs a bi-encoder architecture trained via unidirectional contrastive learning for request-candidate alignment. We conduct extensive evaluations across 11 benchmark suites and 190 tasks spanning multimodal decision-making, understanding (closed-set) and retrieval (open-set), highlighting where MetaEncoder beats SOTA multimodal encoders, as well as its current limits on reasoning-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [16] [RAG-Stress: Probing the Limits of Evidence Reliance in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11183)
*Shunyuan Zhou,Hao Chen,Tianyu Wang,Goose Lin,Zaiyuan Wang,Haiying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAG-Stress 提出受控诊断协议，通过编辑证据断言并交叉来源优先策略与答案位置，测量模型在原本答对时被误导性证据替换答案的误导率 MR。对 15 个系统（API 模型、开源模型、RL 搜索智能体）在 TriviaQA-RC、HotpotQA、SearchQA 与中英文 MedQA 上评测，发现优先文档策略比允许依赖先验知识策略的 MR 高 10.9–13.5 个百分点，平均 MR 呈 End > Beginning > Middle，配对审计显示有害覆盖增加而未带来对应的有益纠正提升，说明证据遵循不等于事实可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成中，遵循检索证据并不保证事实正确：误导性证据可能使模型替换掉原本正确的答案，而标准准确率指标把答案替换与原有错误混在一起，掩盖了这种有害覆盖行为，因此需要区分“证据遵循”与“事实可靠性”。

Method: RAG-Stress 是一个诊断协议而非新模型架构。其流程为：输入固定一个问题与一个参考答案，在不改变问题与正确答案的前提下，对检索证据文本中的单个断言进行编辑，使其支持一个预先指定的错误答案；同时，把答案片段在证据文本中的位置作为受控变量，分别置于证据的开头（Beginning）、中间（Middle）与结尾（End）。协议交叉两种来源优先策略：一种是指令要求优先采信文档证据，另一种允许模型依赖自身先验知识。评测分为两部分：其一，先在无检索条件下让模型作答，筛选出每个模型自身能够正确回答的问题子集，在这些子集上测量误导率 MR，即模型因误导性证据而把原本正确的答案替换为错误答案的比例；其二，在完整评测集上测量干净准确率，以反映整体答题质量。评估覆盖 15 个系统，包括 API 模型、开源模型以及用强化学习训练的搜索智能体，数据集为 TriviaQA-RC、HotpotQA、SearchQA，并额外加入英文与中文 MedQA。另设一个配对审计：在 500 个问题与两个模型 checkpoint 上，检查有害覆盖（模型被误导而改写原本正确回答）的增加是否伴随有益纠正（本来错误、被证据纠正为正确）的同步提升。整套设计的核心在于将答案替换行为与模型自身原有错误解耦，从而把“是否遵循证据”与“答案是否事实正确”两个维度分开测量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "误导率 MR 差距（优先文档策略 vs 允许依赖先验知识策略）", "value": "10.9 至 13.5 个百分点", "setting": "在 TriviaQA-RC、HotpotQA、SearchQA 三个 QA 数据集上，对模型与答案位置取平均"}, {"metric": "平均误导率 MR 的答案位置排序", "value": "End > Beginning > Middle", "setting": "两种来源优先策略下均成立，但单个模型并非一致遵循该顺序"}, {"metric": "配对审计结果", "value": "有害覆盖增加，但未建立对应的有益纠正提升", "setting": "500 个问题、两个 checkpoint 的配对审计"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 RAG-Stress 受控诊断协议：保持问题与参考答案固定，编辑单一断言使其支持指定错误答案；交叉两种来源优先策略（指令优先文档 vs 允许依赖先验知识）与三种答案片段在证据文本中的位置（Beginning/Middle/End）；在模型无检索即答对的子集上测量误导率 MR，并在完整评测集上测量干净准确率，从而把答案替换与原有错误分离；对 15 个系统（API 模型、开源模型、RL 训练的搜索智能体）在 TriviaQA-RC、HotpotQA、SearchQA 及中英文 MedQA 上做交叉评测；对 500 个问题与两个 checkpoint 做配对审计，检验有害覆盖增加是否伴随有益纠正的提升

Abstract: Following retrieved evidence does not guarantee factual correctness: misleading evidence can induce a model to replace an answer it previously gave correctly. Standard accuracy measures obscure this behavior by combining answer replacement with preexisting errors. We introduce RAG-Stress, a controlled diagnostic protocol for examining the limits of evidence reliance in retrieval-augmented generation. The protocol holds the question and reference answer fixed, edits one assertion to support a designated incorrect answer, and crosses two source priority policies with three positions of the answer span within the evidence text. We measure misleading rate (MR) on each model's subset of questions answered correctly without retrieval, alongside clean accuracy on the full evaluation set. We evaluate fifteen systems spanning API models, open models, and search agents trained with reinforcement learning on TriviaQA-RC, HotpotQA, and SearchQA, with additional English and Chinese MedQA evaluations. Instructions that prioritize documents consistently produce higher MR than those permitting reliance on prior knowledge. Averaged over models and positions, the gap ranges from 10.9 to 13.5 percentage points across the three QA datasets. Mean MR follows End $>$ Beginning $>$ Middle under both policies, although individual models do not uniformly follow this ordering. A separate paired audit of 500 questions and two checkpoints supports increased harmful override without establishing a corresponding improvement in beneficial correction. These findings distinguish evidence adherence from factual reliability and motivate evaluating whether retrieved evidence preserves, replaces, or corrects a model's answers.

</details>


### [17] [Can a System-One LLM Perform Knowledge Tracing When Few or No Learners Are Logged?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11135)
*Unggi Lee,Haeun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究现成System-One LLM在极少或没有学习者日志时进行知识追踪的能力。提出Jev：通过官方System-One适配器让LLM单次前向直接输出答题正确概率；Jev零样本在7个数据集上平均AUC .706，超过用8个学习者训练的最佳深度KT（.689）和System-Two Thinking-KT（.650），且API成本约为后者的1/100。加入少量示例和相似学习者统计的JevKT将平均AUC提升至.722，在最多16个学习者时显著领先深度KT，平均领先至64个学习者，监督KT在64到128个学习者之间追平。reader swap与污染检查排除了输入格式或记忆数据的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标平台几乎没有学习者日志（冷启动）时，传统深度知识追踪模型无法训练，而现有基于LLM的System-Two知识追踪方法需要微调或多次采样投票，速度慢且概率粗糙；论文探索现成System-One LLM能否单次前向直接输出答题正确概率，并在少样本甚至零样本条件下完成知识追踪。

Method: Jev架构：输入为typed question，即经过结构化类型标注的题目文本，通过官方System-One适配器直接送入现成（off-the-shelf）的System-One LLM；LLM在一次前向传播中直接输出该题被正确作答的概率值，无需微调、无需采样投票、无需额外推理链。输出概率用于知识追踪，即估计学习者对知识点的掌握程度。JevKT在此基础上引入已记录学习者的少量答题示例以及相似学习者统计量作为额外输入上下文，同样由System-One LLM单次前向融合这些上下文后输出概率。训练目标方面，Jev与JevKT均不进行参数更新，属于零样本/少样本上下文适应；对比方法System-Two Thinking-KT则通过微调目标数据或对10个样本进行推理和投票生成答案，再转化为概率，速度慢且概率粗糙。推理流程上，Jev单次调用API即可获得概率，JevKT在单次调用中附加示例与统计特征；论文通过reader swap（三个其他LLM使用字节完全相同的typed请求）和污染检查验证增益特定于Jev模型本身而非输入格式或记忆数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean AUC", "value": ".706", "setting": "Jev零样本，无目标平台数据，7个数据集平均"}, {"metric": "mean AUC", "value": ".689", "setting": "28个深度知识追踪模型中最佳者，仅用8个学习者训练，7个数据集平均"}, {"metric": "mean AUC", "value": ".650", "setting": "System-Two Thinking-KT，7个数据集平均"}, {"metric": "mean AUC", "value": ".722", "setting": "JevKT加入已记录学习者示例和相似学习者统计量，7个数据集平均"}, {"metric": "显著领先深度KT的学习者数量", "value": "最多16个学习者", "setting": "JevKT与深度KT比较"}, {"metric": "平均领先深度KT的学习者数量", "value": "最多64个学习者", "setting": "JevKT与深度KT比较"}, {"metric": "监督KT追平JevKT的学习者数量区间", "value": "64到128个学习者之间", "setting": "监督KT与JevKT比较"}, {"metric": "API成本相对Thinking-KT", "value": "约1/100", "setting": "Jev对比System-Two Thinking-KT"}, {"metric": "其他三个LLM（相同typed请求经官方System-One适配器）", "value": "在全部7个数据集上均低于Jev", "setting": "reader swap实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Jev：直接使用现成System-One LLM，通过官方System-One适配器以单次前向为目标平台的typed question返回概率，实现零样本知识追踪。；提出JevKT：在Jev基础上加入少量已记录学习者示例和相似学习者统计量，提升少样本适应能力。；系统性比较System-One与System-Two（Thinking-KT）在7个数据集上的效果与约1/100的API成本差异。；通过reader swap（三个其他LLM使用相同typed请求）和污染检查排除输入格式或记忆数据导致增益的解释。；揭示在新学习者首次交互时Jev立即有优势，而在所有学习者已登录的未见题目上深度KT仍然领先的边界条件。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models need many logged learners, so a new course or platform starts without a usable model. In LLM-based KT the LLM generates the answer, which we call System-Two; it is either fine-tuned on the target data or reasons and votes over ten samples, which is slow and gives coarse probabilities. We ask whether an off-the-shelf System-One LLM, which returns a probability for a typed question directly in a single pass, can perform KT when few or no learners are logged. On seven datasets, Jev without any data from the target platform reaches a mean AUC of .706, above the best of 28 deep KT models trained on 8 learners (.689) and above System-Two Thinking-KT on all seven datasets (.650) at about 1/100 of its API cost. Adding examples and a similar-learner statistic from the logged learners (JevKT) raises this to .722; JevKT stays significantly ahead of deep KT up to 16 learners and ahead on average up to 64, and supervised KT catches up between 64 and 128 learners. Among the readers we tested, the gain is specific to Jev, since three other LLMs queried with the byte-identical typed request through the official System-One adapter fall below it on all seven datasets, and reader swaps and contamination checks find no evidence that the input format or memorised data explain the gain. For new learners the advantage holds from their first interactions, whereas on unseen items with all learners logged, deep KT remains ahead.

</details>


### [18] [Stochastic Teacher Intervention for Agentic On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10878)
*Junnan Liu,Linhao Luo,Zhijun Chen,Qianren Mao,Thuy-Trang Vu,Gholamreza Haffari*

TL;DR: STI-OPD 提出随机教师干预框架，用于多轮智能体 on-policy 蒸馏：基于教师-学生策略 KL 差异采样干预概率，在差异大时用教师动作替换学生动作，并用重要性加权反向 KL 目标修正混合策略轨迹的采样失配。在工具集成推理与长时程交互任务上，该方法在全部评测基准和不同学生规模上均优于最强 OPD 基线，消融显示差异引导干预与重要性加权均有贡献。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体任务中，学生模型早期错误会随回合累积，导致轨迹偏离教师 roll-out 分布，使 on-policy 蒸馏的 token 级监督不可靠甚至有害。

Method: STI-OPD 面向多轮智能体 on-policy 蒸馏。输入端为学生模型在交互环境中生成的 roll-out 轨迹，教师模型对学生生成的 token 提供稠密监督。核心模块一为差异引导的教师干预：在多轮交互的每一步，计算学生策略与教师策略之间的 KL 散度作为策略差异度量，并将其映射为干预概率。核心模块二为随机干预策略：从该概率中采样是否干预；若干预，则用教师生成的动作替换学生提议的动作，从而在策略差异大时增加教师控制，在差异小时保留学生探索。干预产生混合策略轨迹，其中部分响应由教师生成。核心模块三为重要性加权反向 KL 目标：由于教师生成响应的 token 采样分布与学生策略存在失配，引入重要性权重修正该失配，使训练目标仍等价于原始 OPD 的 token 级监督，避免偏离蒸馏目标。训练采用学生生成的 roll-out 与混合策略轨迹，通过上述目标更新学生模型。推理时学生模型独立执行多轮工具集成推理与长时程交互，无教师干预。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) efficiently transfers capabilities from a stronger teacher to a student language model through dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts and has shown promise on complex tasks such as mathematical reasoning. However, in multi-turn agentic tasks, student decisions shape subsequent observations, causing early errors to accumulate across turns. The resulting trajectories can drift away from the teacher's rollout distribution, making the teacher's token-level supervision less reliable or even counterproductive for OPD training. To address this issue, we introduce STI-OPD, a stochastic teacher intervention framework for multi-turn agentic OPD. During multi-turn interaction, STI-OPD uses teacher intervention guided by teacher-student policy discrepancy to replace the student's proposed action with a teacher-generated one to maximize the acquisition of reliable supervision. We further develop a stochastic intervention strategy, addressing the limitations of previous threshold-based or fixed-schedule approaches, that estimates policy discrepancy using KL divergence and maps it to an intervention probability. By sampling whether to intervene from this probability, STI-OPD adaptively balances teacher control with student exploration. To learn from the resulting mixed-policy trajectories, we introduce an Importance-Weighted Reverse KL objective that corrects the token sampling mismatch between teacher-generated responses and the student policy to preserve the original OPD objective. Across tool-integrated reasoning and long-horizon interaction, STI-OPD outperforms the strongest prior OPD baseline on every evaluated benchmark and student size. Ablations further show that both discrepancy-guided intervention and importance weighting contribute to these gains.

</details>


### [19] [Sparse Attention Is Matrix Approximation, Not Choosing from a Bag of Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10871)
*Fang Wan,Xufeng Liu,Fan Li,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出主流稀疏注意力误把注意力矩阵当作“一袋值”来挑选，忽略其作为结构化矩阵参与 A·V 乘积的本质，提出 MASA：用闭式评分衡量每个稀疏单元对矩阵乘积近似误差的贡献，可作为即插即用修正接入现有稀疏注意力框架而不改内核与预算，并在多种方法、基准与 backbone 上取得一致精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法把注意力矩阵当作“一袋标量值”，只保留大数值或高注意力质量区域，忽略了注意力矩阵作为一个结构化矩阵通过与 value 向量相乘共同决定输出的本质，导致稀疏近似误差未被正确度量。

Method: 论文以标准注意力为出发点：注意力输出由注意力矩阵 A 与 value 矩阵 V 相乘得到，因此 A 是参与矩阵乘法的结构化矩阵，而非独立可丢弃的标量集合。现有稀疏注意力按标量大小或质量排名保留 query-key 交互，等价于对 A 做条目级取舍，未考虑这些条目在 A·V 矩阵乘积中的联合作用。MASA 将稀疏选择重新表述为矩阵近似：对每个候选稀疏单元，计算其纳入后对矩阵乘积近似误差的闭式减少量，并以此作为排序分数替代原始的注意力质量排序。MASA 被设计为一种插件式修正，只替换排序/选择准则，不改变原有稀疏注意力框架的稀疏内核形态与计算预算，因此可直接叠加在已有稀疏注意力方法上。推理路径上，模型先按原框架生成候选稀疏单元，再用 MASA 的闭式近似误差分数进行选择，最后执行原有稀疏注意力计算并输出。论文通过跨多种稀疏注意力方法、多个基准和多个 LLM backbone 的实验验证一致精度提升，以支持 MASA 与矩阵近似视角。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出稀疏注意力应被建模为矩阵近似问题，而非从一袋注意力条目中挑选最大值；提出 MASA（Matrix Approximation Sparse Attention），用闭式评分衡量每个稀疏单元对矩阵乘积近似误差的降低程度；MASA 作为理论驱动的即插即用修正，可在不改变现有稀疏注意力框架的稀疏内核与预算的前提下接入

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many domains, but their efficiency is limited by the quadratic cost of attention with respect to prompt length. Sparse attention reduces this cost by retaining only a small fraction of query-key interactions to approximate the full attention matrix. However, existing methods are trapped in a mathematically wrong view: they simply keep large scalar entries or high-mass regions of the attention matrix. This treats the attention matrix as a bag of values, ignoring that it is used as a structured matrix whose entries jointly determine the attention output through multiplication with value vectors. We argue that this is the core conceptual issue: sparse attention should be formulated as matrix approximation, not as blindly choosing the largest values from a bag of entries. Based on this view, we propose Matrix Approximation Sparse Attention (MASA). MASA replaces raw attention-mass ranking with a closed-form score that measures how much each sparse unit reduces matrix-product approximation error. As a theory-grounded plug-in correction, MASA can be added to existing sparse attention frameworks without changing their sparse kernels or budgets. Extensive experiments across multiple sparse attention methods, benchmarks, and LLM backbones show consistent accuracy gains, supporting both MASA and the matrix-approximation view of sparse attention.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示式前沿大模型能从课程转录中提供语法掌握视图，但在全平台学习者规模下成本过高；需要一种低成本、高精度的替代方案，在不损失概念识别、证据跨度定位和正确性判断精度的前提下大幅降低服务成本。

Abstract: Corrective feedback is among the best-evidenced drivers of second-language acquisition, yet corrections delivered during lessons rarely accumulate into an actionable view of grammar mastery. Prompted frontier models can provide such a view from learner--tutor lesson transcripts, but they are costly at scale. We close this gap by fine-tuning Qwen3.5 small language models (SLMs) on filtered and rebalanced teacher-generated supervision, then deploying an efficient 0.8B model in an end-to-end grammar mastery tracker for all English learners on our platform. Internalizing the annotation contract into adapter weights enables pairing the 0.8B model with a compact matched prompt rather than verbose instructions. On two human-curated benchmarks, both the deployed 0.8B model and a 4B reference comparator outperform prompted GPT-5.4 and GPT-5.6 Sol in precision and recall under nested matching criteria of increasing strictness: concept, evidence span, and correctness. The deployed 0.8B SLM reduces serving cost by approximately 16$\times$. A feature-level online experiment shows significant gains in learner engagement ($+15.8\%$) and key business metrics, including scheduled hours ($+2.1\%$) and GMV from new lessons ($+13.2\%$).

</details>

*SAIL Model Team,Boyuan Sun,Bryan Dai,Che Liu,Chi Liu,Derek Li,Hongming Piao,Mengzhuo Chen,Xidong Wang,Yan Shu,Yinda Chen,Ziyang Zeng*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何以远小于主流开放权重模型的参数量，构建在文献检索与证据选择、科学编码推理、以及长程研究规划与修订上具备竞争力的科学智能体模型。

Method: SAIL 是一个总参数 35B、激活参数 3B 的开放模型，面向文献研究、科学编码与多步骤研究工作流。其开发采用科学感知的改进闭环：先用基于前沿 AI 模型的智能体对 SAIL 的任务失败进行诊断，分别检查文献任务中的搜索与证据选择、科学编码中的科学假设与推理、以及长程调研中的规划与修订；随后这些智能体利用论文集合与科学代码仓库构造训练任务，生成问题、交互轨迹以及带有必要环境与工具的可执行任务；该闭环在多个开发周期中重复。训练方面，SAIL 依次经过监督微调、专家训练、多教师 on-policy 蒸馏和智能体强化学习。推理时模型以智能体形式执行科学工作流，调用环境与工具完成检索、代码执行与多步调研，但摘要未披露具体模块结构、延迟或吞吐数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "科学科研任务整体表现", "value": "与领先开放权重模型相比具有竞争力（论文未披露具体数值）", "setting": "论文报告范围，具体基准、基线与数值未在给定摘要中披露"}, {"metric": "参数效率", "value": "35B 总参数、3B 激活参数，显著少于领先开放权重模型", "setting": "模型规模对比，未披露具体基线模型与评测数值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 科学感知的闭环改进流程：由前沿模型的智能体分析 SAIL 的任务失败，定位文献任务中的搜索与证据选择、编码中的科学假设与推理、长程调研中的规划与修订等能力缺口。；由上述诊断自动构造针对性训练任务，包括问题、交互轨迹与可执行任务，并配套环境与工具。；多轮开发循环叠加多阶段训练：监督微调、专家训练、多教师 on-policy 蒸馏与智能体强化学习。；以 35B 总参数、3B 激活参数实现相对领先开放权重模型具有竞争力的科研任务表现。

Abstract: We introduce SAIL, an open model with 35B total and 3B active parameters for literature research, scientific coding, and multi-step research workflows. SAIL is developed through a science-aware improvement loop: agents built on frontier AI models analyze its task failures and construct training tasks that address the underlying capability gaps. The diagnosis examines search and evidence selection in literature tasks, scientific assumptions and reasoning in coding, and planning and revision in longer investigations. The agents draw on paper collections and scientific code repositories to build problems, interaction trajectories, and executable tasks with the required environments and tools. We repeat this loop over multiple development cycles and train SAIL through supervised fine-tuning, specialist training, multi-teacher on-policy distillation, and agentic reinforcement learning. SAIL achieves competitive performance across scientific research tasks with substantially fewer parameters than leading open-weight models.

</details>


### [22] [Gated Memory: Admission-Controlled Memory Formation for Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11270)
*Preeti Saraswat,Divya Neelagiri,Ajay Manoj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Gated Memory，在对话事实写入向量存储之前加入“准入门+条件丰富”两个检查点：先在完整话语上下文中判断候选事实是否值得写入，再分解为原子事实并做分类、出处标注、作用域限定与时空接地，且禁止断言上下文中不存在的实体；在 LoCoMo-10 上以相同检索与生成组件取得 +2.6% 相对 LLM-judge 准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时记忆系统的“形成阶段”（事实首次写入存储的时刻）缺乏原则性设计：永久属性与临时情境等关键上下文信号只在原始话语中存在，一旦抽取成主-关系-宾三元组即不可逆丢失，后续任何下游处理都无法恢复，从而成为记忆质量的瓶颈。

Method: Gated Memory 在对话与持久化存储之间插入两个决策检查点。输入是当前对话交换（utterance/exchange）以及此前轮次；此前轮次仅作为只读参考上下文使用。第一阶段为准入门（admission gate）：对每一个候选事实，在事实抽取（extraction）运行之前，结合完整原始话语上下文进行准入判断，输出一个结构化的形成记录（formation record）；该门只针对当前交换作出决策，避免历史信息干扰当前判断。第二阶段为条件丰富（conditional enrichment）：对被接纳的内容，先分解为原子事实（atomic facts），再对每条事实进行分类、标注出处（区分“直接陈述”与“推断”）、限定其适用条件的作用域（scope to condition of applicability），并将时间与地点解析并接地（grounded in resolved time and place）；整个丰富过程受实体作用域分类法（entity scope taxonomy）与隐私约束控制，硬性约束是不得断言任何未出现在上下文中的实体。最终被接纳且丰富后的记忆记录写入持久化向量存储，下游检索与生成阶段与基线保持一致，从而把“形成质量”隔离为一个可测量的变量。训练目标/优化细节未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LLM-judge accuracy", "value": "+2.6% (relative)", "setting": "LoCoMo-10 benchmark，与强基线使用相同的检索与生成组件，仅改变记忆形成阶段"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Gated Memory形成框架，在对话与存储之间插入两道决策检查点：准入门（admission gate）与条件丰富（conditional enrichment）；准入门在抽取之前基于完整话语上下文评估每个候选事实，仅评估当前轮次，同时将历史轮次作为只读参考上下文，并产出结构化的形成记录；条件丰富阶段通过实体作用域分类法（entity scope taxonomy）对接纳事实进行接地，并施加隐私约束：不得断言上下文中不存在的实体；接纳内容被分解为原子事实，逐条分类、标注出处（直接陈述 vs 推断）、限定适用条件的作用域、并接地到已解析的时间与地点

Abstract: Personalized conversational AI relies on long-term memory systems that extract facts from user utterances and store them in persistent vector stores. Despite progress in retrieval, deduplication, and lifecycle management, the formation stage, the moment a fact is first written to storage has received almost no principled attention. We identify this as the binding constraint on memory quality in production systems. Critical contextual signals, such as the distinction between a permanent user attribute and a transient situation, exist only in the original utterance and are irreversibly lost the moment extraction produces a subject-relation-object triple. No downstream process can recover them. We propose Gated Memory, a lightweight, modular formation framework that interposes two decision checkpoints between conversation and storage: an admission gate that evaluates every candidate fact against the full utterance context before extraction runs, and a conditional enrichment stage that grounds admitted facts through an entity scope taxonomy with privacy constraints. The gate evaluates only the current exchange while using prior turns as read-only reference context, and produces a structured formation record. Admitted content is decomposed into atomic facts, each categorized, tagged with provenance (directly stated versus inferred), scoped to its condition of applicability, and grounded in resolved time and place, subject to a constraint that no entity absent from the context may be asserted. On the LoCoMo-10 benchmark with atypical emotional density in utterance data, Gated Memory achieves an overall +2.6% relative improvement in LLM-judge accuracy over a strong baseline with identical retrieval and generation, establishing formation quality as a measurable constraint on memory performance.

</details>
