TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性环境下预算约束在线学习的预算平滑问题，提出了达到近乎最优遗憾界的全信息算法，并扩展到在线资源分配，首次实现 o(√T) 遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性环境下，针对任意预算平滑策略类，如何实现近乎最优的遗憾界，以有效控制预算消耗。

Method: 论文考虑在线学习框架：学习者在每轮选择预算平滑策略，与环境交互并观察奖励与花费。给定 F 个专家的集合和一个候选预算平滑计划，目标是使累计花费接近该计划，同时最大化奖励。算法采用全信息反馈，通过在线学习技术（如跟随正则化领导者）结合对花费偏差 D 的惩罚，实现对抗性遗憾界。算法扩展至在线资源分配场景，其中学习者可观察到当前选项的奖励和成本，并允许分数分配。理论分析建立了匹配的下界，并证明在分数分配下可达到 o(√T) 遗憾。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出全信息算法，在任意专家类下，对抗性设置中实现相对于任意预算平滑策略的遗憾界 O(D√logF + √T logF)。；证明该技术可扩展到在线资源分配问题，在允许分数分配时达到 O(D√logF) 遗憾界。；首次实现此类任务的 o(√T) 遗憾保证。

Abstract: We establish near-optimal regret bounds for budget-constrained online learning against arbitrary classes of budget-pacing experts in the adversarial setting. In particular, given any class of $F$ experts and a candidate budget pacing schedule, we provide a full-information algorithm which obtains regret $O(D \sqrt{\log F}+ \sqrt{T\log F})$ against all experts whose cumulative spending stays within distance $D$ of this schedule, matching lower bounds established by Braverman et al. (2025). We additionally show that our technique extends to various problems in online resource allocation, where the learner gets to see the rewards and costs of the current options available to them, and establish $O(D\sqrt{\log F})$ regret bounds when fractional allocation is allowed. This is the first algorithm we are aware of which can achieve $o(\sqrt{T})$ guarantees for such tasks.

</details>


### [49] [QUILT: Rethinking Sparse-Attention Prefill through Shared Query Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11134)
*Zhenduo Zhao,Qihui Zhou,Mingcong Song,Zhiyi Chen,Chuangguan Ye,Fengfan Hou,Zequn Gong,Jing Li,Hongjie Si,Guoping Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUILT 通过共享查询执行优化稀疏注意力预填充：利用相邻查询 KV 选择重叠，以 SCSD 将不规则集合操作规则化，并在多粒度级联共享与 tile 感知调度下减少冗余 KV 加载和计算。在 LongBench 上用 GLM-5.3、DeepSeek-3.2 评测，内核延迟最高降低 55.1%，处理 KV 数据最高减少 55.9%，TTFT 最高降低 36.8%，精度几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力内核独立处理每个查询，导致相邻查询选择的共享 KV 条目被反复加载和解量化，造成冗余内存流量与计算开销，长上下文预填充成本高。

Method: QUILT 是一种面向工作负载感知的稀疏注意力执行机制，针对长上下文预填充阶段，将相邻查询联合处理而非独立处理。输入是查询集与 KV cache；观察到相邻查询选择的 KV 条目存在大量重叠。核心模块 SCSD（Shift-and-Compare Set Decomposition）将不规则的集合选择/比较操作转换为规则的数据并行原语，使其能高效映射到现代加速器；SCSD 与注意力计算流水线化，以隐藏集合分解带来的额外开销。级联共享（Cascaded sharing）在多个粒度上分层捕获 KV 复用，从而扩大跨查询共享范围。tile 感知执行策略在共享粒度与硬件 tile 利用率之间进行权衡，并有选择地移除低重要度的查询特有尾部，避免出现未充分利用的 tile，提高有效吞吐。最终执行流程为：选择共享 KV 条目、以 SCSD 生成规则化集合表示、流水线执行注意力计算，并在 tile 级别调度以最大化复用与硬件利用率。论文在 LongBench 上使用 GLM-5.3 与 DeepSeek-3.2，在 tensor parallelism 和 sequence parallelism 下评估，报告内核延迟、处理 KV 数据量以及 TTFT 延迟的降低，且精度退化可忽略。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average kernel latency", "value": "up to 55.1% reduction", "setting": "与最先进的稀疏注意力内核对比，在 LongBench 上使用 GLM-5.3 和 DeepSeek-3.2，tensor parallelism 与 sequence parallelism 两种设置下"}, {"metric": "processed KV data", "value": "up to 55.9% reduction", "setting": "与最先进的稀疏注意力内核对比，在 LongBench 上使用 GLM-5.3 和 DeepSeek-3.2，tensor parallelism 与 sequence parallelism 两种设置下"}, {"metric": "time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency", "value": "up to 36.8% reduction", "setting": "与最先进的稀疏注意力内核对比，在 LongBench 上使用 GLM-5.3 和 DeepSeek-3.2，tensor parallelism 与 sequence parallelism 两种设置下；精度退化可忽略"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Shift-and-Compare Set Decomposition (SCSD)：将不规则集合操作转换为规则的数据并行原语，适配现代加速器。；跨查询共享 KV 条目：联合处理相邻查询并复用共享 KV，减少冗余内存流量和计算。；级联共享（Cascaded sharing）：在多个粒度上分层捕获 KV 复用。；tile 感知执行策略：平衡共享粒度与硬件 tile 利用率，并选择性移除低重要度的查询特有尾部以消除未充分利用的 tile。；将 SCSD 与注意力计算流水线化以隐藏其开销。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long-context attention, but existing kernels typically process queries independently, repeatedly loading and dequantizing KV entries shared across queries. We observe substantial overlap in the KV entries selected by neighboring queries, creating opportunities for cross-query reuse. We present QUILT, a workload-aware sparse-attention execution mechanism that jointly processes neighboring queries and reuses shared KV entries to reduce redundant memory traffic and computation. QUILT introduces Shift-and-Compare Set Decomposition (SCSD), which transforms irregular set operations into regular data-parallel primitives suitable for modern accelerators, and pipelines SCSD with attention computation to hide its overhead. Cascaded sharing captures reuse hierarchically at multiple granularities. A tile-aware execution strategy balances sharing granularity with hardware tile utilization and selectively removes low-importance query-specific tails to eliminate underutilized tiles. We evaluate QUILT on LongBench using GLM-5.3 and DeepSeek-3.2 under both tensor and sequence parallelism. Compared with the state-of-the-art sparse-attention kernel, QUILT reduces average kernel latency by up to 55.1% and processed KV data by up to 55.9%, while reducing time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency by up to 36.8% with negligible accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [50] [Dynamics as Code: On Model Compression via Dynamic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11115)
*Fan Gao,Wei Su,Juntong Fan,Renfeng Peng,Hongyu Liu,Jinqiao Duan,Feng-Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将模型压缩视为动态系统轨迹编码的新范式，统一四类动态系统，证明无理缠绕轨迹构成权重空间的ε-net，在ResNet-18和Qwen系列模型上实现无需后训练的压缩，并提供KD-tree加速与离群点控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存和计算约束下，如何将预训练神经网络的高维权重压缩为紧凑表示，并在解压时可控地恢复，避免后训练微调。

Method: 框架将神经网络权重向量视为高维空间中的点，通过动态系统生成的轨迹来编码：给定动态系统（如无理缠绕、Hilbert曲线、Lorenz系统等），权重向量被映射到轨迹上的最近状态索引，压缩时仅存储该索引；解压时根据索引从轨迹中恢复状态向量，作为原始权重的近似。理论部分证明在Diophantine条件下，无理缠绕的有限轨迹M=O(ε^{-(d+ν)})个状态构成d维权重空间的ε-net，从而确保解压误差可控，并给出压缩率与状态分辨率的解析关系。为扩展到大模型，引入KD-tree和坐标模板加速最近邻搜索，并通过离群点识别限制最大误差。训练目标不涉及网络训练，而是无损或有损地编码已有权重，实现无需后训练微调的压缩。推理时，权重可动态解压或缓存恢复后的权重用于前向计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "压缩率", "value": "在ResNet-18、Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen1.5-7B上取得有竞争力的压缩率，无需后训练", "setting": "相同解压误差约束下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将模型压缩重新概念化为动态系统轨迹的索引编码，与剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、低秩分解本质不同。；在Diophantine条件下证明无理缠绕的有限轨迹构成权重空间的ε-net，建立状态分辨率、解压误差与压缩率的可预测关系。；统一四类动态系统（空间填充曲线、混沌系统、同余/伪随机生成器、低差异序列）作为压缩框架。；引入KD-tree和坐标模板加速以扩展到大模型，并利用离群点识别控制误差。

Abstract: The escalating size of pretrained neural networks has rendered model compression a prerequisite for deployment under stringent memory and compute constraints. With the irrational winding as an example, earlier work introduced a dynamic system (DS) paradigm that reconceptualizes compression as compact weight representation: high-dimensional parameters are encoded by the index of a trajectory produced by a dynamic system, from which the vector is recovered during decompression. This mechanism is fundamentally distinct from pruning, quantization, knowledge distillation, and low-rank decomposition. Along this direction, we prove that under a Diophantine condition, a finite trajectory of \(M = O(\epsilon^{-(d+\nu)})\) states in the irrational winding constitutes an \(\epsilon\)-net over the \(d\)-dimensional weight space, thereby linking state resolution, decompression error, and compression ratio in a predictable manner. Furthermore, we propose a generalized DS-based model compression framework by unifying four DS families---space-filling curves (Hilbert, Peano, Morton/Z-order, Snake), chaotic systems (Lorenz), congruential and pseudo-random generators (LCG, PCG), and low-discrepancy sequences (Halton). Also, we introduce the KD-tree and coordinate-template acceleration to scale to large models as well as outlier identification to control the error. Experiments on ResNet-18 and Qwen2.5-1.5B/Qwen1.5-7B validate that DS-based compression achieves competitive compression ratios without post-hoc retraining, with controllable decompression error and flexible state-space design, establishing it as a principled and practical compression approach.

</details>


### [51] [Stability-Plasticity Balance via Singular-Vector Selection in LLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11076)
*Lingxiang Wang,Hainan Zhang,Liang Pang,Hongwei Zheng,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SVC，以奇异向量通道为单元，通过估计适配收益和遗忘成本并自适应选择可训练通道，实现 LLM 持续学习中稳定性与可塑性的平衡。在四个 LLM 家族、八个下游任务上优于现有 PEFT 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在领域特定持续适配中面临灾难性遗忘，如何在获得新能力与保持预训练能力之间取得平衡，现有 PEFT 方法缺乏原则性的可训练单元来分配可塑性与稳定性。

Method: SVC 首先利用领域特定数据估计每个奇异向量通道的适配收益，同时利用固定的公开通用领域语料作为历史激活代理来估计遗忘成本；然后基于这些分数通过膝点代价筛选、帕累托前沿过滤和 Otsu 阈值化自适应地选择可训练通道；最后仅对选中的通道进行持续微调，从而在获取新知识的同时保留预训练能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "下游性能与预训练能力保留", "value": "优于现有 PEFT 基线", "setting": "四个 LLM 家族、八个下游任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别奇异向量通道作为管理稳定性-可塑性权衡的自然单元；提出 SVC 方法，基于领域数据估计通道的适配收益，基于通用语料估计遗忘成本；自适应通道选择流程：膝点代价筛选、帕累托前沿过滤、Otsu 阈值化

Abstract: Domain-specific continual adaptation of LLMs risks catastrophic forgetting, creating a fundamental tension between acquiring new capabilities and preserving those learned during pretraining. PEFT mitigates this problem by restricting the number of trainable parameters, but existing methods lack a principled unit for deciding where plasticity should be allocated and stability should be preserved. We identify the singular-vector channel as a natural unit for managing this trade-off. Each channel represents an input-output transformation, which can be updated to acquire new knowledge or fixed to preserve pretrained capabilities. Based on this perspective, we introduce SVC, a parameter-efficient continual-learning method that selectively updates Singular-Vector Channels. Before fine-tuning, SVC uses domain-specific data to estimate each channel's adaptation benefit and a fixed public general-domain corpus only as a history activation proxy for estimating forgetting cost. It then adaptively selects trainable channels based on these scores via knee-based cost screening, Pareto-front filtering, and Otsu thresholding. Experimental results across four LLM families and eight downstream tasks show that SVC better preserves pretrained capabilities while achieving strong downstream performance relative to existing PEFT baselines. Further analysis of channel scoring and selection demonstrates that selective plasticity at the singular-vector-channel level enables effective continual LLM adaptation.

</details>


### [52] [Measuring and Mitigating Solution Mode Collapse in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11064)
*Liv G. d'Aliberti,Marwa Abdulhai,Sofiia Druchyna,Peter Henderson,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现RLVR后训练会导致解模式坍缩，即正确解多样性下降；提出ModeBench基准量化该现象，并设计Re:Max重放策略，通过存储并均匀训练每个已发现模式，跨三种模型规模提升成功率与解多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR后训练倾向于将概率集中到少数正确解模式上，导致解模式坍缩（solution mode collapse），即使准确率持平或提升，模型输出多样性也下降；前沿模型已表现出高度集中现象。

Method: 论文未披露完整架构细节，仅从摘要可知：使用带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对语言模型进行后训练，其奖励函数仅判断正确性而不区分解模式；ModeBench基准在多解任务上运行验证器，同时输出正确性标签与所发现的解模式标签，用于测量训练过程中解多样性的变化。Re:Max方法在RLVR训练循环中加入重放缓冲区：每当验证器发现一个新正确解模式时，将其对应示例存入缓冲区；训练时从缓冲区中均匀采样各模式示例进行策略更新，从而对抗概率向少数模式集中的倾向。训练目标仍为RLVR目标，但数据分布被重放缓冲区均匀化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: ModeBench：首个同时返回正确性与解模式的多解任务基准，用于量化解多样性；Re:Max：为每个已发现模式在重放缓冲区中存储一个已验证示例，并均匀训练这些存储模式，使一次发现的解与多次发现的解获得同等训练权重

Abstract: A language model (LM) can usually answer the same question in more than one way, but reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is indifferent to which correct answer a model produces. A solution will earn the same reward whether it is the thousandth copy of a familiar answer or one the model has never produced before. Yet, there is potential value in having the model retain multiple correct solutions as it is trained. For instance, multiple modes may give users a choice and provide problem-solving strategies that improve overall model performance. Here, we introduce ModeBench, a benchmark of multi-solution tasks in which the verifier returns both correctness and mode discovered. We then use ModeBench to measure how solution diversity changes under RLVR post-training. We find that RLVR post-training concentrates probability onto fewer correct modes even as accuracy holds or improves, and moreover, that frontier models are already highly concentrated. We then introduce our solution, Re:Max, which stores one verified example per discovered mode in a replay buffer and trains on those stored modes uniformly. A solution found once is, therefore, practiced as often as one found repeatedly. Across three model scales, two RL objectives, and harder task constructions, replay improves both how often a policy succeeds and how many different ways it can succeed.

</details>


### [53] [Optimizing Large Language Models with Chained LMOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10975)
*Sungyoon Kim,Kaan Ozkara,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出链式 LMO 统一框架解释 Muon 族优化器的有效性，并设计 TensorChain 优化器，通过跨层堆叠权重矩阵成 3D 张量并沿轴归一化，在 Qwen3 0.6B/1.7B 预训练中比 Muon 平均节省 9.6% token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 启发的优化器通过组合多个矩阵归一化取得了成功，但这些方法碎片化且缺乏统一视角；许多组合超出标准 LMO 框架并在光滑凸目标上可能发散，需要解释组合为何有效并提出更好的优化器。

Method: 论文将 Muon 类优化器抽象为链式线性最小化预言机（LMO）的组合。每个 LMO 对权重矩阵执行归一化操作（如谱范数归一化等），链式 LMO 则是按顺序组合多个这样的归一化。TensorChain 作为该框架下的新优化器，将来自不同层的兼容权重矩阵堆叠成一个 3 维张量，然后沿该张量的不同轴进行归一化，从而跨层耦合权重更新。训练时，优化器利用链式 LMO 对梯度进行变换后更新参数。论文还通过线性联想记忆模型分析了链式组合相比单步 LMO 在各向异性嵌入下的优势，给出了组合改善的直觉。推理路径与标准 Transformer 前向传播相同，优化器仅影响训练过程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均 token 节省率", "value": "9.6%", "setting": "Qwen3 0.6B 和 1.7B 预训练，在匹配验证损失下，平均 token 效率相对 Muon 的提升"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出链式线性最小化预言机（chained LMOs）统一框架，将现有 Muon 类优化器视为 LMO 的组合。；通过线性联想记忆分析，证明在各向异性嵌入下链式组合可以优于 Muon，为组合的有效性提供理论解释。；提出 TensorChain 优化器，堆叠不同层的兼容权重矩阵并对 3D 张量沿各轴归一化，在 Qwen3 预训练中超越所有链式基线。

Abstract: Muon has motivated a growing family of optimizers that compose multiple matrix normalizations, but these methods remain fragmented and lack a unified perspective. We introduce chained linear minimization oracles (chained LMOs), which cast these methods as compositions of LMOs. Despite their empirical success, many chains fall outside the standard LMO framework and can diverge on smooth convex objectives. To explain why composition can nevertheless help, we turn to linear associative memory and show that chaining can improve over Muon under anisotropic embeddings. Empirically, we propose TensorChain, a novel optimizer within the framework that stacks compatible weight matrices across different layers and normalizes the 3d tensor across its axes. In Qwen3 0.6B and 1.7B pretraining, TensorChain outperforms all chained baselines in average token efficiency, with average token savings of 9.6% over Muon at matched validation loss.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现代LLM的通用知识蒸馏方法缺失，现有工作多集中于后训练阶段的能力蒸馏，缺乏对通用知识蒸馏的系统研究。

Method: 未提供详细架构说明。根据摘要，KDFP是一种白盒通用知识蒸馏方法，利用教师模型的表示训练学生模型，并通过短暂参数减少提升训练效率。具体模块、数据流、训练目标及推理路径未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "1.6% – 4.9%", "setting": "在9个基准上对比现有方法"}, {"metric": "训练效率提升", "value": "最高99.1%", "setting": "通过短暂参数减少实现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次从第一性原理出发，系统评估并整合先前经验，设计面向现代LLM的通用白盒知识蒸馏方法 KDFP；提出短暂参数减少（ephemeral parameter reduction）机制，显著提升训练效率

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is an established technique for improving the capabilities of small, efficient student models by training them with the representations of larger, more capable teacher models. Much of the recent work in the distillation of large language models (LLMs) has focused on distilling abilities learned during post-training, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning, and tool usage. This has left a large research gap in general knowledge distillation for LLMs, which is essential for developing efficient and private systems suitable for deployment on edge devices. We take a first-principles approach, evaluating previous lessons from prior works and conducting new explorations to develop a distillation methodology suitable for modern LLMs. We present KDFP, a novel methodology for white-box general knowledge distillation in LLMs. We demonstrate that KDFP outperforms existing methods by 1.6% $-$ 4.9% across 9 benchmarks while increasing training efficiency by up to 99.1% through ephemeral parameter reduction.

</details>


### [55] [Language Models for Page-Level Layout Decisions in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10920)
*Varun Joshi,Eva C. Song,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究用语言模型离线评估电商搜索页面级布局决策（是否在特定位置插入次级商品堆栈），比较直接提示、提示衍生特征和表示方法，发现基于表示的方法在预测用户参与度上一致优于基于提示的方法，为电商搜索离线布局评估提供了可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统搜索评价方法（如 interleaving）不适用于页面级布局变化，而在线 A/B 测试成本高昂；论文研究离线方法，评估给定布局决策（是否在特定位置插入次级堆栈）是否对用户有益。

Method: 论文比较三类语言模型评估方法用于预测页面布局决策（是否在特定位置插入次级商品堆栈）对用户参与度的影响。直接基于提示的方法将布局描述直接输入语言模型，提示其判断是否有利；基于提示衍生特征的方法先从模型输出中提取特征，再用于预测用户参与度；基于表示的方法利用语言模型的内部表示（如隐藏状态或嵌入），作为特征输入到下游预测模型。实验结果表明，基于表示的方法在预测用户参与度方面一致优于基于提示的评判方法，为电商搜索离线布局评估提供了可靠基础。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将语言模型作为可扩展的离线评估器，用于电商搜索页面级布局决策；系统比较直接基于提示、基于提示衍生特征和基于表示的方法在预测用户参与度上的效果；发现基于表示的方法在离线评估中一致优于基于提示的评判方法

Abstract: E-commerce search pages are critical touchpoints for millions of online shoppers. While traditional search engines return a ranked list of results, modern E-commerce search pages increasingly incorporate recommender system modules -- for example, secondary stacks that surface alternative product groupings at specific positions. When introduced appropriately, secondary stacks can improve user engagement; however, suboptimal placement may disrupt browsing flow and degrade the primary results. Unlike traditional search ranking, where evaluation techniques such as interleaving are well established, evaluating page-level layout changes e.g., when and where to insert a secondary stack remains challenging without costly online A/B testing. To address this, we study offline methods for evaluating whether a given layout decision -- specifically, the inclusion of a secondary stack at a particular position -- is beneficial to users. We investigate language models as scalable evaluators by comparing direct prompt-based, prompt-derived feature, and representation-based methods. Our results show that representation-based approaches consistently outperform prompt-based judging in predicting user engagement, suggesting they provide a reliable foundation for offline layout evaluation in E-commerce search.

</details>


### [56] [Amortized Off-Policy Evaluation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10848)
*Younwoo Choi,Leo Feng,Vincent Liu,Haanvid Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PFN-OPE提出一种摊销式离线策略评估方法，通过在包含策略偏移与奖励偏移的上下文赌博机任务分布上预训练先验数据拟合网络，测试时单次前向传播即可估计LLM候选策略价值，在HelpSteer2与UltraFeedback上误差比最佳基线低2.0至9.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典OPE方法按任务定义、每遇新日志数据或新奖励函数需从头拟合，无法适应LLM持续部署中同时出现的策略偏移与奖励偏移。

Method: PFN-OPE是一种先验数据拟合网络（PFN），将离线策略评估摊销到上下文赌博机任务分布上。输入为已记录的数据集（上下文、动作、奖励）以及每个提示的一个采样目标响应；网络在预训练阶段接触大量由LLM响应池构造、并由多个奖励函数打分的任务，从而同时暴露于策略偏移（策略更新导致响应分布偏离日志）与奖励偏移（奖励定义随业务变化）。PFN-OPE通过一次前向传播输出价值估计，避免了经典OPE方法每个任务或新数据集从头拟合的需求。训练目标是在任务分布上学习从日志数据与目标响应到价值估计的映射，测试时无需逐任务优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "误差降低倍数", "value": "2.0至9.3倍低于最佳基线", "setting": "在HelpSteer2与UltraFeedback数据集上，使用Qwen、Llama、Gemma策略，所有测试配置的奖励偏移设置下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出PFN-OPE，用先验数据拟合网络在上下文赌博机任务分布上摊销OPE；在构造的包含策略偏移与奖励偏移的任务池上一次性预训练；测试时单次前向传播即可将日志数据集与每提示一个目标响应映射为价值估计，无需按任务拟合

Abstract: Accurate evaluation is central to selecting which LLM to deploy, yet testing a candidate on live traffic exposes real users to an unvetted model. Teams therefore evaluate candidates offline, on data produced by already-deployed models. This is off-policy evaluation (OPE), and it faces two distribution shifts: as a model is updated in post-training, its responses diverge from the logged ones (policy shift), and the reward definition under which it is judged changes with business requirements (reward shift). Classical OPE methods are ill-suited to this continual-deployment setting because they are defined per task and require fitting from scratch on every new logged dataset or reward definition. To address this, we propose PFN-OPE, a prior-data fitted network that amortizes OPE across a distribution of contextual-bandit tasks. We pretrain it once on tasks constructed from a pool of LLM responses scored by several reward functions, in which both shifts occur. At test-time it maps a logged dataset and one sampled target response per prompt to a value estimate in a single forward pass, with no per-task fitting. On HelpSteer2 and UltraFeedback with Qwen, Llama, and Gemma policies, PFN-OPE achieves 2.0 to 9.3 times lower error than the best baselines across all tested configurations in the reward-shifted settings.

</details>


### [57] [Evaluating Rubric Generation with Interventional Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10809)
*Erik Skalnes,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Interventional Transfer方法评估LLM生成的评分标准，通过干预响应观察不同评分标准是否一致变动。在HealthBench案例研究中发现生成与专家评分标准之间存在不对称性，对使用LLM生成评分标准进行性能监控和爬山优化有启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在大规模场景下有效评估LLM生成的评分标准（rubric）的质量，尤其是在有专家评分标准作为参考的情况下。

Method: 该方法基于干预转移（Interventional Transfer）的思想：给定两个评分标准（如生成的rubric和专家rubric），通过扰动一个响应使其在其中一个rubric上通过或失败，然后评估该扰动是否能在另一个rubric上产生一致的效果。如果两个rubric相似，那么当响应被扰动以通过/失败其中一个时，它们在另一个上的评分也应相应地变化。论文在HealthBench数据集上进行案例研究，使用Qwen3.8-27B、Deepseek-V4-Flash和Opus-5生成rubric，并用于评估GPT-5.6-Terra的响应，观察扰动转移的不对称性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Interventional Transfer (IT)评估方法，通过干预响应并观察两个评分标准是否一致变动来判断它们的相似性；证明不同形式的干预转移可用于评估生成评分标准在不同任务中的效用；在HealthBench案例研究中发现了生成评分标准与专家评分标准之间的不对称性：导致分数降低的扰动可转移，而导致分数升高的扰动不可靠转移

Abstract: Instance-specific rubrics are common in AI benchmarks where reliable evaluation requires specific expert knowledge. This approach is difficult to scale, prompting research into the generation of rubrics with large language models (LLMs). However, even when expert rubrics are available as references, it is unclear how to productively evaluate the quality of generated rubrics at scale. In this paper, we introduce a method for the evaluation of rubric generation, which we call Interventional Transfer (IT), based on the idea that two rubrics are similar if they move together when a response is perturbed to pass/fail one of them. In contrast to existing approaches for evaluation of rubric generation, we argue that different forms of interventional transfer can be used to evaluate the utility of generated rubrics for different tasks. For instance, we apply this approach in a case study on HealthBench, where we demonstrate an asymmetry in rubrics generated by Qwen3.8-27B, Deepseek-V4-Flash, and Opus-5, used to evaluate responses from GPT-5.6-Terra. Perturbations that degrade responses according to the generated/expert rubric transfer into lower scores on the corresponding expert/generated rubric, but perturbations that improve on one rubric do not reliably transfer into higher scores on the other. We argue that this finding has implications for the usage of LLM-generated rubrics for performance monitoring and hill-climbing. We contrast our approach with existing approaches for rubric evaluation, which do not surface the same asymmetry that we observe.

</details>

### [58] [UniData: Universal Multimodal Instruction Generation Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11363)
*Jiaqi Tang,Yi-Feng Wu,Yuting Zhang,Hao Lu,Bowen Fu,Qing-Guo Chen,Xiaogang Xu,Yuwei Hu,Shiyin Lu,Wei Wei,Lei Zhang,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Qifeng Chen,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniData提出多模态指令生成流水线，将用户需求扩展为多样事件，结合任意到任意大模型生成多轮多模态指令，并利用轮次相关性进行质量优化。同时构建了覆盖9种模态的2万条UniDataset。实验显示其数据质量达SOTA，并能提升其他多模态模型的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的高质量指令数据构建成本高，现有生成方法存在模态支持有限、难以生成多轮指令的问题。

Method: UniData流水线包含三个阶段：首先，将用户需求扩展为多个多样化事件；然后，利用任意到任意（any-to-any）大模型基于事件生成多模态指令；最后，通过利用指令轮次间的相关性，纠正无关和冗余的推理流程，从而提升数据质量。为训练该流水线，构建了UniDataset数据集，包含2万条、9种模态的样本。实验表明UniData在数据质量上达到SOTA，并能增强其他多模态模型的理解和生成能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出UniData通用指令生成流水线，将简单用户需求转化为多轮、多模态指令。；构建UniDataset数据集，涵盖2万条、9种模态。；利用指令轮次间的相关性纠正无关和冗余推理流程，提升数据质量。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly being applied in a wider range of real-world scenarios. However, due to the substantial labor cost, creating high-quality multimodal instruction datasets for MLLMs remains a significant challenge. Although some methods propose to generate instruction data, they often face limitations in modality support and struggle with generating multi-round instructions. To address these problems, we introduce UniData, a universal instruction generation pipeline, to transform simple user requirements into multi-round, multimodal instructions. Specifically, UniData first expands user requirements into multiple diverse events. Using these events, UniData then integrates an any-to-any large model for multimodal instruction generation. Finally, UniData enhances data quality by correcting irrelevant and redundant inference flow, leveraging correlations between instruction rounds. To train this pipeline, we also build UniDataset, a dataset comprising 20,000 entries across nine modalities for improved multimodal generation. Our experiments demonstrate that UniData achieves SOTA performance in data quality and can also enhance the understanding and generation capabilities of other multimodal models.

</details>


### [59] [Safe, Persistent, and Evolving Agent Harness for Understanding Partially Observable Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11552)
*Yisen Gao,Yue Guo,Qing Zong,Yiwen Guo,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: E-Ledger 是一个面向企业工作流的多智能体执行 harness，通过代码审批层保障动作合规、通过世界账本持久跟踪隐藏规则与动态状态，并用 WorldAbduct 溯因进化机制从四个视角诊断轨迹、假设并验证潜在规则；在企业基准 World of Workflows 上以四个 LLM 骨干实现安全任务完成率较最强基线提升 5–15 个百分点，并在科学环境验证迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的企业工作流中，LLM 代理需要严格合规执行动作、发现隐藏规则与副作用，并在长程任务中维持持久且经证据支持的状态。

Method: E-Ledger 是一个多智能体执行 harness，输入为待执行的企业工作流任务与工具调用提议。其核心包含两层：(1) code approval layer，在每个提议动作真正执行前对照组织政策进行检查，拦截不合规动作；(2) world ledger，持久记录两类信息——已验证的隐藏规则，以及带证据支撑的动态状态，从而在部分可观测条件下维持跨记录的长程状态。由于隐藏规则通常先验未知，系统进一步引入 WorldAbduct 进行溯因式 harness 进化：它从四个互补视角诊断执行轨迹——状态一致性、世界-观测差距、政策门正确性、目标判断——据此假设潜在规则，并通过定向的溯因交互验证假设，验证通过后整合进世界账本，使 harness 随执行不断演化。训练与推理资源未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "safe task completion", "value": "较最强进化基线提升 5–15 个百分点", "setting": "World of Workflows 企业基准，四个 LLM 骨干模型"}, {"metric": "safe task completion", "value": "论文未给出具体数值", "setting": "ScienceWorld 与 DiscoveryWorld 科学环境"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: code approval layer：在执行前将每个提议动作与政策进行校验；world ledger：维护已验证隐藏规则与证据支持动态状态；WorldAbduct：从状态一致性、世界-观测差距、政策门正确性、目标判断四个视角诊断执行轨迹，溯因假设潜在规则并通过定向交互验证；将溯因式 harness 进化从企业工作流迁移到科学环境（ScienceWorld、DiscoveryWorld）

Abstract: Large language model agents can invoke tools fluently, but enterprise workflows demand more than selecting the right tools: actions must strictly comply with organizational policies, tool feedback often conceals hidden side effects under partial observability, and long-horizon tasks require persistent state tracking across multiple records. To address these challenges, we introduce E-Ledger, a multi-agent harness for safe and persistent execution. E-Ledger employs a code approval layer that checks every proposed action against policy before execution, and maintains a world ledger of verified hidden rules alongside evidence-backed dynamic state. Because hidden rules are typically unknown a priori, we further propose WorldAbduct, an abductive, world-model-driven harness evolution framework. WorldAbduct diagnoses execution trajectories across four complementary views (state consistency, world-observation gap, policy-gate correctness, and goal judgment) to hypothesize latent rules, and verifies them through targeted abductive interactions before integrating them into the ledger. On the enterprise benchmark World of Workflows, E-Ledger with WorldAbduct improves safe task completion across four LLM backbones, outperforming the strongest evolution baseline by 5--15 percentage points. Experiments in ScienceWorld and DiscoveryWorld further show that abductive harness evolution carries over to scientific environments. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [60] [ReTeach: Building a Self-Teacher through Multi-Round Reflection and Retry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11529)
*Yafeng Tang,Hao Li,Hongsheng Yu,Qiang Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTeach提出一种无需外部参考答案的自蒸馏框架，通过多轮反思与重试构建自教师，并采用结果感知的加权蒸馏损失，在六个推理基准上平均准确率比GRPO提升1.39个百分点，推理时保持单次前向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在没有外部参考答案、任务反馈或跨示例记忆的条件下，通过自生成尝试的多轮反思与重试构建有效的自教师，从而提升学生模型的推理能力。

Method: ReTeach从一次失败的学生rollout开始，教师模型（同一基础模型）进行显式反思，产生错误诊断和修正指导，然后重新尝试；每次失败的重试都用于下一次反思，直到成功或重试预算耗尽。不依赖参考答案、外部诊断反馈或跨示例记忆。在收集到教师上下文后，采用结果感知的选择与加权策略：将样本分为初次正确、反思纠正和未解决三类，对类别归一化蒸馏损失赋予不同权重。学生通过on-policy蒸馏，在自己rollout的前缀上匹配教师上下文条件下的token级预测分布。训练完成后，学生以单次推理模式部署，无需教师或反思过程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "比GRPO提升1.39个百分点", "setting": "六个基准（数学推理、科学问答、工具使用）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 仅利用自生成尝试和结果级验证，通过多轮反思与重试构建自教师上下文；结果感知的选择与加权策略，区分初次正确、反思纠正和未解决样本，并分别加权类别归一化蒸馏损失；在策略蒸馏中让学生匹配教师上下文条件下的token级预测分布，同时保持单次推理

Abstract: Self-distillation can improve reasoning without a separately trained, more capable teacher, but its effectiveness depends on how the self-teacher gains an advantage over the student. Conditioning the teacher on reference answers or solutions can provide such an advantage, but this information may be unavailable. Reflection offers a way to derive explicit error diagnoses and revision guidance from self-generated attempts, yet existing reflection-based methods often combine it with reference information, rich task feedback, or persistent memory. We introduce ReTeach, a Reflective self-distillation framework that constructs its self-Teacher through multi-round reflection and retry using only self-generated attempts and outcome-level verification. Starting from an unsuccessful student rollout, the teacher alternates explicit reflection with renewed attempts until success or the retry budget is exhausted, without reference answers or solutions, external diagnostic feedback, or cross-example memory. Each failed retry informs subsequent reflection, while successful correction provides outcome-level evidence for the potential utility of the resulting teacher context. An outcome-aware selection and weighting strategy distinguishes initially correct, reflection-corrected, and unresolved examples, assigning separate weights to their category-normalized distillation losses. Through on-policy distillation, the student matches the teacher's context-conditioned token-level predictive distributions at prefixes of its own rollouts, transferring the benefits of iterative correction while retaining single-pass inference. Across six benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, science question answering, and tool use, ReTeach improves average accuracy over GRPO by 1.39 percentage points.

</details>


### [61] [Balancing Reference Guidance and Free Generation in Trajectory Rollouts for Reasoning RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11128)
*Hanyu Wang,Nakul Agarwal,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Makoto Fukushima,Jinghui Chen,Vaishnav Tadiparthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究推理RL中参考解引导与自由生成的平衡问题，通过前缀续写的概率分析提出自适应参考引导（ARG），在GRPO全失败组中构造可验证正确轨迹。在Qwen3-4B/8B和五个数学基准上，ARG取得最高aggregate pass@12和具有竞争力的平均采样准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推理强化学习中，如何平衡参考解的引导与模型自由生成，以在固定生成预算下构造更多可验证的正确轨迹，从而提升GRPO等策略优化算法的训练效果。

Method: 论文提出自适应参考引导（ARG）方法，用于推理强化学习中的轨迹生成。输入为参考解（已验证的正确轨迹）和当前策略模型；在训练时，对每个问题，模型可选择从参考解的前缀继续生成（prefix continuation），生成完整轨迹后经外部验证器校验，若通过则采用该轨迹，否则回退到参考解本身。由于不同前缀长度影响续写成功概率和参考意外度（reference surprisal），论文通过理论分析（一次续写的KL散度闭式）得到引导量的权衡关系，并据此学习一个共享的前缀选择器（prefix selector），该选择器跨训练问题从续写结果中学习，无需估计成功概率或额外生成。最终ARG在固定生成预算内构造正确轨迹，并应用于GRPO算法的全失败组（all-failure groups）：当某个问题组内所有采样轨迹均失败时，ARG利用参考前缀引导生成正确轨迹，从而为组内提供正奖励信号，更新策略。推理时模型直接生成，无需参考引导。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "aggregate pass@12", "value": "最高（相对于评估的其他方法）", "setting": "Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-8B，五个数学推理基准"}, {"metric": "average sampled accuracy", "value": "有竞争力（competitive）", "setting": "Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-8B，五个数学推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过前缀续写概率分析，推导出一次续写情况下与理想分布的KL散度闭式表达，揭示了参考引导量与续写成功概率、参考意外度之间的权衡。；提出自适应参考引导（ARG）方法，共享前缀选择器跨问题学习续写结果，无需估计成功概率或额外生成，在固定生成预算内构造正确轨迹。；将ARG集成到GRPO的全失败组处理中，利用参考引导生成可验证轨迹以缓解组内奖励全零问题。

Abstract: A verified reference solution provides a correct trajectory for training a reasoning model. Alternatively, a prefix of the reference can guide the model in generating a trajectory of its own. How much reference guidance should we provide? We study this question through prefix continuation, where the model continues from a reference prefix and keeps the resulting trajectory if it passes verification, falling back to the reference otherwise. Since both procedures produce correct trajectories, we compare their distributions with the ideal distribution, the model's own distribution conditioned on successful verification. For one continuation, we derive the KL divergence in closed form, which, up to a bounded term, decreases with the product of the probability of generating a different correct trajectory and the reference surprisal, the negative log probability of the reference suffix given the prefix. Since a longer prefix tends to raise the former but lowers the latter, continuation success alone does not determine the preferred amount of guidance. From this analysis, we learn a prefix selector shared across training questions from continuation outcomes, without estimating success probabilities or additional generation. The resulting Adaptive Reference Guidance (ARG) constructs correct trajectories within a fixed generation budget, and we apply it to all-failure groups in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Experiments on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that ARG achieves the highest aggregate pass@12 among the evaluated methods with competitive average sampled accuracy.

</details>


### [62] [What Output-Only Review Cannot Verify: Study Contracts for Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11754)
*Eitan Waks,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“研究契约”框架，用于验证AI研究智能体的输出与执行过程，通过绑定批准与实际执行证据来识别仅凭产物无法检测的缺陷。诊断实验表明确定性检查器可检出全部注册变异，而多法官评估在信息受限下产生大量弃权与失败。该工作为LLM智能体的可靠性验证与生产化部署提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何验证AI生成的研究中哪些缺陷可以通过产物本身识别，哪些必须依赖执行前已批准的信息，从而建立一种契约相对而非绝对科学的验证机制。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：1) 研究契约，定义实验选择、运行义务和声明范围；2) 执行证据记录，包括批准与执行对象的摘要（digests）；3) 确定性检查器，根据注册的故障特定规则，在批准与执行对象上检测缺陷。诊断实验使用八个自制的干净/变异对，检查器对全部八个注册变异均检测成功。此外，将十八个记录法官别名（judge aliases）分别给予经过元数据过滤的包（不含配对上下文或注册表选择的故障标签），在144个变异评估案例中，104例收到缺陷标记，剩余案例包括32次弃权和8次终端失败，无对变异案例的明确干净判定。提示词指示法官在证据不足时弃权。整个流程强调信息不对称设置，未进行全信息对比或闭环智能体评估。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出研究契约（study contracts）概念，将声明的实验选择、运行义务和声明范围与记录的执行证据绑定；严格区分契约相对验证与科学真理，明确验证的信息边界；设计确定性检查器，通过注册的故障特定规则在批准与执行对象上检测变异，并进行了诊断性实验

Abstract: Some defects in an AI-generated study can be identified from its artifacts; others require knowledge of what was approved before execution. We propose study contracts that bind declared experimental choices, run obligations and claim scope to recorded execution evidence, and distinguish this contract-relative verification from scientific truth. A diagnostic using eight self-authored clean/mutated pairs illustrates the information boundary. A deterministic checker applying a registered, fault-specific rule to approved and executed objects detected all eight registered mutations. Across eighteen recorded judge aliases given individual metadata-filtered packages without pair context or the registry-selected fault label, 104 of 144 mutated evaluation cases received defect flags; the remaining cases comprised 32 abstentions and eight terminal failures, with no explicit clean decisions on mutated cases. Some packages retained approval and execution fields, including digests. The prompt instructed judges to abstain when evidence was insufficient. These results characterize a deliberately information-asymmetric development setting; they do not isolate the effect of authoritative information from differences in task specification and rule selection, and they are not comparative verifier quality or agent reward hacking. We identify full-information comparisons, legitimate-adaptation controls and closed-loop agent evaluations as necessary tests of whether contract checks improve useful compliant completion under optimization.

</details>


### [63] [Use and Disuse: Intent-Structured Experience Consolidation for Memory and Learning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12124)
*Xiangyi Zeng,Baihang Liu,Xutong Wang,Ze Jin,Yunpeng Li,Qixu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hippocam 借鉴人类记忆的选择性维持与重复巩固特性，为 LLM Agent 提出嵌套 intent 的分层记忆架构，通过递归前缀合并实现经验抽象、召回与再巩固，使 Agent 无需参数更新即可从自身经验中持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 从单任务执行走向长期自主运行时，如何将连续经验转化为可复用知识，并在有限上下文中保持当前目标相关信息，同时支持长期记忆的渐进抽象、召回与更新。

Method: Hippocam 是一个分层记忆与持续学习架构。输入为 Agent 持续执行任务过程中产生的交互经验（ongoing work），这些经验被组织为嵌套的 intent 结构：当前活跃 intent 构成工作上下文的主体，已完成 intent 不再携带完整工作细节，而是被压缩为后续工作所需的任务相关结果与状态。架构包含递归前缀合并机制：对较早历史反复执行合并，使长期未被使用的经验逐层抽象；同时保留原始交互记录，使得当需要更细粒度信息时，Agent 可沿层级逐步恢复细节，并在信息足够时停止回溯。当过去经验被召回并重新整合进活跃工作时，它们会与新的经验一起经历后续合并，从而被强化、补充和更新。该记忆动态形成“用进废退”的机制，把工作上下文、长期记忆、知识积累和技能学习统一到一个持续演化的经验过程中，使 Agent 可以在不更新模型参数的情况下通过自身经验学习并进化能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 以嵌套 intent 结构组织 Agent 的持续工作流，使活跃上下文聚焦当前 intent，完成后的 intent 只保留后续任务相关的结果与状态。；递归前缀合并机制反复合并较早历史，使长期未使用的经验逐步抽象化，同时保留原始交互以支持按需恢复细节。；将被召回并重新整合进活跃工作的过往经验再次参与后续合并，形成 use and disuse 的记忆动态，实现经验强化、补充与更新。；在同一持续经验过程中连接工作上下文、长期记忆、知识积累与技能学习，使 Agent 无需参数更新即可从自身经验中学习与进化。

Abstract: The evolution of Large Language Model agents from single-task execution to long-term autonomous operation highlights the critical challenge of transforming continuous experiences into reusable knowledge. To address this, we propose Hippocam, a hierarchical memory and continual learning architecture. Hippocam draws inspiration from two characteristics of human memory: cognitive processes selectively maintain information relevant to current goals, while long-term memories form gradually through repeated consolidation. Accordingly, Hippocam structures an agent's ongoing work as nested intents. The active context remains centered on the current intent, while completed intents are consolidated into the task-relevant outcomes and state needed for subsequent work, rather than carrying forward their full working details. Concurrently, a recursive prefix consolidation mechanism repeatedly consolidates earlier history, causing long-unused experiences to become increasingly abstract. Original interactions are preserved, allowing the agent to progressively recover finer-grained details through the hierarchy and stop once sufficient information is available. Crucially, when past experiences are recalled and reintegrated into active work, they undergo subsequent consolidation alongside new experiences, thereby being reinforced, supplemented, and updated. Through this memory dynamic of use and disuse, Hippocam connects working context, long-term memory, knowledge accumulation, and skill learning within a single continuously evolving experiential process. This enables agents to learn and evolve capabilities through their own experiences without parameter updates.

</details>


### [64] [Universal Textual Teaching for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12114)
*Zhanyi Lu,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出UTT，一种无需参数更新的LLM知识蒸馏框架，将Teacher-Student知识差距转化为可解释、可复用的自然语言Primer。在数学与代码生成任务上大幅提升Student表现，并展现出跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏需要训练Student参数，导致知识绑定于特定架构与检查点，难以解释、复用，且不适用于API-only或训练成本高昂的模型。

Method: UTT是一个免参数更新的知识蒸馏框架。输入为需要蒸馏的特定任务（如数学推理或代码生成）以及一对Teacher-Student模型。框架首先通过配对评估识别Student与Teacher之间存在显著性能差距的代表性案例。然后进入多角色迭代循环：Student模型尝试解决每个案例并产生输出；Prompter角色将评估反馈转化为教学指令；Teacher模型基于该指令提供针对性的演示；Synthesizer角色整合已验证的经验，形成自然语言产物Primer。Primer可反复迭代更新，最终作为可复用的文本知识注入到Student的上下文中，无需更新Student的任何参数。推理时，Student直接使用Primer辅助完成任务，实现了跨模型、跨检查点的知识迁移与复用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "9.4% -> 48.6%", "setting": "KernelBench代码生成任务"}, {"metric": "Fast1 accuracy", "value": "9% -> 35%", "setting": "KernelBench代码生成任务"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "27.6% -> 51.7%", "setting": "Omni-MATH-2数学推理任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出参数更新自由的Universal Textual Teaching (UTT)框架，将Teacher-Student知识差距蒸馏为自然语言产物Primer。；通过配对评估识别代表性差距案例，设计多角色交互循环：Student尝试、Prompter生成教学指令、Teacher提供针对性示范、Synthesizer整合验证过的经验。；Primer可解释、可复用，且为一个Teacher-Student对合成的Primer能泛化到未参与合成的其他Student。；在数学与代码生成任务上超越代表性提示工程和基于参数的KD方法。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from stronger Teacher models to weaker Student models, but most methods require training the Student parameters, thereby binding the distilled knowledge to a specific architecture and checkpoint. This implicit representation is difficult to interpret or reuse across models and limits KD for API-only or costly-to-train models. This paper studies knowledge transfer for large language models (LLMs). We introduce Universal Textual Teaching (UTT), a parameter-update-free framework that distills observed Teacher-Student knowledge gaps into a textual, interpretable, and reusable natural-language artifact called Primer. Specifically, UTT first identifies representative gap cases through paired evaluations, and iteratively updates the Primer via multi-role interactions: the Student attempts each task, the Prompter turns evaluation feedback into a teaching instruction, the Teacher provides a targeted demonstration, and the Synthesizer consolidates validated lessons. Empirically, on the challenging math (Omni-MATH-2) and code generation (KernelBench) tasks, extensive results confirm the effectiveness of the method: UTT remarkably raises the Student's accuracy from 9.4% to 48.6% and Fast1 accuracy from 9% to 35% on KernelBench, while increasing mathematical reasoning accuracy from 27.6% to 51.7%. UTT also performs better than representative prompt engineering and parameter-based KD methods. Of note, UTT is shown to be generalizable across different Teachers and Students: a Primer synthesized for one Teacher-Student pair can generalize to other Students that do not participate in the synthesis.

</details>


### [65] [EvoAlloc: A Self-Evolving Resource Allocation Agent for Efficient Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12086)
*Yanning Dai,Yuhui Wang,Nanbo Li,Wenyi Wang,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoAlloc 提出一种自进化资源分配智能体，通过经验整合与反事实探索动态调整LLM程序进化中的评估资源分配，在达到基线性能时减少59-82%全量评估和61-89% LLM token，并在同预算下提升最终性能8.7-12.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的程序进化中评估计算成本高昂，固定资源分配策略无法根据搜索进展动态调整，导致低价值候选浪费资源而高潜力候选被忽视。

Method: EvoAlloc 是一个自进化的资源分配智能体，嵌入在 LLM 程序进化循环中。输入为当前搜索迭代产生的候选程序及其相关搜索上下文；主模块包括：(1) 分配器：基于已有经验决定每个候选应获得的评估计算资源（如是否进行全量评估）。(2) 经验整合模块：周期性汇总先前的搜索与分配结果，形成可复用的经验表示，用于指导后续策略修订。(3) 反事实探索机制：当分配器对某些候选不分配资源时，偶尔强制评估这些候选，将其真实结果作为反事实反馈补充到经验中，避免策略僵化。(4) 策略修订：利用整合后的经验更新分配器的决策逻辑。训练/优化目标是通过经验迭代最小化达到高水平程序所需的评估开销，同时最大化最终程序性能。推理时，在每一轮搜索中，分配器先产生资源分配决策，仅对获得资源的候选执行全量评估，被拒候选通常跳过评估，但按概率触发反事实评估；评估结果回流到经验池，驱动下一轮策略更新。最终在编码和智能体框架优化基准上验证了评估次数与 token 消耗的降低以及最终性能的提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "全量评估次数减少", "value": "59-82%", "setting": "达到基线水平性能所需的全量评估次数"}, {"metric": "总LLM token减少", "value": "61-89%", "setting": "达到基线水平性能所需的总LLM token消耗"}, {"metric": "最终性能提升", "value": "8.7-12.0%", "setting": "在相同的全量评估预算下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 自进化资源分配智能体：周期性将历史搜索与分配结果整合为可复用经验，用于修订后续分配策略。；反事实探索机制：偶尔评估被分配器拒绝资源的候选，将其结果纳入经验以更新策略。；在编码与智能体框架优化基准上实现显著降低全量评估次数与LLM token消耗，并在相同全量评估预算下提升最终性能。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution relies on evaluation feedback to guide the iterative search for high-performing programs. However, evaluation is often computationally expensive, making it essential to allocate limited resources to candidates that can most effectively advance the search. Existing LLM-based methods typically rely on fixed allocation strategies throughout the search, potentially wasting resources on low-value candidates while overlooking promising ones. We propose EvoAlloc, a self-evolving resource-allocation agent that learns from search experience to revise its strategy for allocating computational resources across candidates. EvoAlloc periodically consolidates prior search and allocation outcomes into reusable experience, which informs subsequent strategy revisions. It further uses a counterfactual exploration mechanism to occasionally evaluate candidates denied resources by the allocator, revealing their outcomes to enrich its experience for future strategy updates. Across coding and agent-harness optimization benchmarks, EvoAlloc requires 59-82% fewer full evaluations and 61-89% fewer total LLM tokens to reach baseline-level performance. Moreover, under the same full-evaluation budget, EvoAlloc achieves 8.7-12.0% higher final performance.

</details>


### [66] [OnTrack: Real-Time Monitoring and Intervention in LLM Agent Trajectories via Streaming Structure-Aware Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12375)
*Babak Barazandeh,Connor Swanson,Chinmay Kulkarni,Nikhil Mungel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OnTrack提出一种流式LLM Agent轨迹监控机制，利用结构感知最优传输将当前步骤与依赖同历史成功运行比对，每步约1毫秒即可检测计划违规、循环与停滞并阻断Agent；在SWE-bench轨迹上对失败轨迹排序的AUROC比内容相似度方法高0.057，abort策略可节省约18%失败运行的算力，83%的中断为正确拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent安全保障要么依赖额外的safeguard agent监控（每步增加成本与延迟），要么仅在运行结束后做日志后验评估（此时算力已消耗且不可逆操作已发生），缺乏低成本、低延迟的流式监控与即时干预手段。

Method: OnTrack是一套流式监控机制，输入为LLM Agent执行过程中逐步产生的步骤日志、步骤间的依赖结构，以及（在可用情况下）历史成功运行记录与工具schema。其核心是将当前轨迹的结构化表示（步骤序列及其依赖关系）与参考轨迹的结构化表示进行比较，采用结构感知最优传输度量二者之间的差异，从而判断当前步骤是否偏离已知成功模式。系统按数据访问程度分三种模式运行：完整参考访问时同时利用历史运行与工具schema进行计划违规检测；中间访问时仅用工具schema约束当前步骤的合法调用范围；无先验知识时仅依赖逐步生成的日志，通过模式检测识别循环、停滞和重复工具调用等异常。监控在每一步执行时在线进行，一旦检测到偏离或异常即向用户告警或直接阻断Agent。论文还引入abort策略：在运行早期基于轨迹异常程度预测最终失败可能性，并据此提前终止运行以节省算力。评估在SWE-bench轨迹上进行，比较依据前8步对失败与成功轨迹的排序能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC（失败轨迹排序优于成功轨迹）", "value": "+0.057", "setting": "基于前8步的SWE-bench轨迹，与内容相似度方法对比"}, {"metric": "算力节省", "value": "约18%", "setting": "采用abort策略时，相对失败运行本会消耗的算力"}, {"metric": "中断正确率", "value": "83%（6次中断中5次正确）", "setting": "abort策略下被中断的失败运行占比，5/6次abort正确"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出OnTrack流式监控机制，将Agent当前步骤与依赖和已记录的成功运行进行比对，每步约1毫秒即可告警或阻断；将轨迹结构（步骤与依赖）建模为结构感知最优传输（structure-aware optimal transport）相似度度量；定义并系统研究三种数据访问递减的监控范式：完整参考访问（历史运行+工具schema）、中间访问（仅工具schema）、无先验知识（仅逐步生成的日志）；在无参考访问时退化为识别循环、停滞与重复工具调用等异常模式；基于前若干步的失败轨迹排序并结合abort策略实现算力节省

Abstract: Agents are deployed in applications from trip planners and stock trading to IT incident triage. In most cases, LLM agents work autonomously with minimal rule-based safeguarding, leading to cost and safety issues from irreversible actions. Recent works resolve this either by using a safeguard agent to monitor behavior or evaluating logs post-hoc. The first adds cost and latency to every step; the second delivers its verdict after the run, when tokens are burned and damage is done. To overcome this, we propose OnTrack, a streaming monitoring mechanism that compares an agent's steps and dependencies against recorded successful runs to alert users or block the agent in about a millisecond per step. We study this problem in three regimes of decreasing access: full reference access (historical runs and tool schemas), intermediate access (only tool schemas), and no prior knowledge (only step logs as generated). Expectation of OnTrack's monitoring capabilities reduces as data access drops, ranging from plan violation detection to identifying loops, stalls, and repeated tool calls. Finally, we evaluate OnTrack using SWE-bench trajectories. Based on the first 8 steps, our method ranks failing trajectories below succeeding ones better than content similarity approaches (+0.057 AUROC). With an abort policy, we save about 18% of compute that would be burned on failing runs, where 83% of interrupted runs were actually heading to failure (5 out of 6 aborts were correct).

</details>


### [67] [Accurate but Not Humble: Evaluating Epistemic Humility in LLM Agents under Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12360)
*Kaiser Sun,Bernal Jimenez Gutierrez,Hongjun Liu,Jingyu Zhang,Jie Gao,Mark Dredze,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出评估LLM Agent认知谦逊（EH）的轨迹级框架ISE，通过受控和自然知识冲突实验，发现高任务准确率并不代表高EH，Agent常在早期检测到冲突但后期未能解决或传达不确定性，模型级干预可改善EH但牺牲准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent评估主要关注任务成功率，缺乏对Agent在检索证据与先验知识冲突时如何处理不确定性的评估。

Method: 论文提出一个轨迹级评估框架，用于评估LLM Agent在知识冲突下的认知谦逊（EH）。框架将EH操作化为三个行为维度：Identify（识别冲突）、Solve（尝试解决冲突）、Escalate（在无法解决时沟通不确定性）。评估设置包括受控知识冲突（骨干模型的参数知识与检索证据矛盾，或两个上下文来源不一致）和自然多步Agent执行中出现的冲突，每种冲突设置都配有匹配的无冲突对照组。评估流程记录Agent的完整执行轨迹，分析其在不同步骤中是否检测、维持和解决冲突，并检查最终答案是否传达了未解决的不确定性。对四个Agent的评估显示，任务准确率与EH不相关，且模型级干预可以改变EH但影响任务准确率。该框架不涉及特定的模型训练或推理优化，而是提供一种行为评估方法。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务准确率与EH不相关", "value": "高准确率配置不一定具有高EH", "setting": "评估四个Agent在受控和自然知识冲突场景下的表现"}, {"metric": "模型级干预效果", "value": "可提升EH但常导致任务准确率下降", "setting": "对骨干模型进行干预的实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出epistemic humility（认知谦逊）的轨迹级评估框架，包含Identify、Solve、Escalate三个行为维度。；设计受控知识冲突和自然多步执行中的知识冲突两种设置，并配对无冲突对照组。；发现任务准确率与认知谦逊不必然相关，高准确率配置可能在早期检测到冲突但未在最终答案中传达不确定性。；展示模型级干预可以提升认知谦逊但常以任务准确率下降为代价。

Abstract: When retrieved evidence contradicts an agent's prior beliefs, does it revise its answer, acknowledge uncertainty, or persist with an incorrect conclusion? Existing evaluations of agentic systems focus primarily on task success, offering limited insight into how agents handle such conflicts. We propose to evaluate agents on epistemic humility (EH): the agent's willingness to recognize, act on, and communicate uncertainty during task execution. We operationalize EH through three trajectory-level behavioral dimensions: Identify, Solve, and Escalate (ISE). Through knowledge conflict, situations where the backbone language model's parametric knowledge contradicts the evidence it encounters, or where two contextual sources disagree, we evaluate two conflict settings: (1) controlled conflict and (2) naturally occurring conflict during multi-step agentic execution, each paired with matched no-conflict controls. Evaluating four agents, we find that higher task accuracy does not necessarily correspond to greater epistemic humility: some high-accuracy configurations recognize conflicts during execution but do not communicate unresolved uncertainty in their incorrect final answers. Trajectory-level analysis further reveals that agents frequently detect conflicts in early steps of execution but fail to maintain or resolve them in later steps. Finally, we show that model-level interventions can improve EH, but often at the cost of task accuracy, suggesting that epistemic humility emerges from the interaction among the backbone model, agent harness, and evaluation environment.

</details>

*Haohui Wang,Jiahao Xu,Wangzhi Zhan,Tong Zeng,Dongqi Fu,Hong Li,Swastik Roy,Naren Ramakrishnan,Chris North,Jian Kang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*
